#!/usr/bin/env bash # Terry 工具箱：启动检测依赖；确认后才安装，不自动升级系统。 set -u TOOLBOX_DIR='' # No external modules in the standalone bundle. TOOLBOX_VERSION='0.2.0-dev' # 非 root 时自动提权，便于一条命令 `bash <(curl -fsSL sh.uw.cx)` 直接可用。 if (( EUID != 0 )) && [[ -z ${TOOLBOX_ELEVATED:-} ]]; then command -v sudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || { printf '需要 root 权限：请用 root 运行，或先安装 sudo。\n' >&2 exit 1 } export TOOLBOX_ELEVATED=1 case ${BASH_SOURCE[0]:-} in /dev/fd/*|/dev/stdin|/proc/self/fd/*|bash|'') # 流式执行时脚本本身在已关闭/被读空的 fd 上，重新取一份完整副本再提权。 command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1 || { printf '需要 curl 才能自动提权。\n' >&2; exit 1; } _tb_tmp=$(mktemp "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/toolbox.XXXXXXXX") || exit 1 if ! curl -fsSL --proto '=https' --proto-redir '=https' https://sh.uw.cx >"$_tb_tmp"; then printf '下载脚本失败（sh.uw.cx）。\n' >&2 exit 1 fi # 管道执行时 stdin 不是终端，菜单会读不到；能拿到终端就切回去。 if [[ -t 0 ]]; then exec sudo -E bash "$_tb_tmp" "$@" elif { : /dev/null; then exec sudo -E bash "$_tb_tmp" "$@" /dev/null 2>&1; then columns=$(tput cols 2>/dev/null) || columns=80; fi [[ $columns =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || columns=80 (( columns > 78 )) && columns=78 (( columns < 32 )) && columns=32 TB_WIDTH=$((columns - 2)) TB_OS='Linux' TB_OS_ID='unknown' local line val release=${TOOLBOX_OS_RELEASE:-/etc/os-release} if [[ -r $release ]]; then while IFS= read -r line; do case "$line" in PRETTY_NAME=*|ID=*) val=${line#*=}; val=${val#\"}; val=${val%\"}; val=${val#\'}; val=${val%\'} # OS metadata is parsed as data; strip anything that could move the cursor. val=${val//[^a-zA-Z0-9 ._()\/+-]/} case "$line" in PRETTY_NAME=*) TB_OS=$val;; ID=*) TB_OS_ID=$val;; esac ;; esac done < "$release" fi TB_ARCH=$(uname -m 2>/dev/null) || TB_ARCH='unknown' TB_TERRY_ART=( '██████ █████ █████ █████ ██ ██' ' ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ██' ' ██ ████ █████ █████ ███ ' ' ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ' ' ██ █████ ██ ██ ██ ██ ██ ' ) } _tb_display_width() { local LC_ALL=C local text=${1//$'\xef\xb8\x8f'/} i char n=0 # Byte-based so alignment holds under LC_ALL=C or a C-locale SSH login. # Box drawing (U+2500 block, 3 bytes) is one column; only CJK and emoji are two. for ((i=0; i<${#text}; i++)); do char=${text:i:1} case "$char" in [$'\x80'-$'\xbf']) ;; # continuation byte [$'\xe3'-$'\xe9']) ((n+=2));; # CJK ideograph / CJK punctuation [$'\xe2']) case ${text:i+1:1} in [$'\x98'-$'\x9f']) ((n+=2));; *) ((n+=1));; esac ;; [$'\xef']) ((n+=2));; # fullwidth forms [$'\xf0'-$'\xf3']) ((n+=2));; # emoji [$'\xc2'-$'\xdf']) ((n+=1));; # Latin-1 punctuation (·, …) *) ((n+=1));; esac done TB_TEXT_WIDTH=$n } _tb_repeat() { local n=$1 ch=$2 i; for ((i=0;i= 46 )); then _tb_center ' ' for art in "${TB_TERRY_ART[@]}"; do _tb_center "$art" "$TB_BOLD$TB_BLUE"; done _tb_center ' ' _tb_center 'T E R R Y T O O L B O X' "$TB_BOLD$TB_CYAN" else _tb_center ' ' _tb_center 'TERRY TOOLBOX' "$TB_BOLD$TB_CYAN" fi _tb_center '服务器工具箱 · 启动先检测依赖' "$TB_DIM" _tb_sep if (( TB_WIDTH >= 60 )); then _tb_center "$TB_OS $TB_ARCH $TOOLBOX_VERSION" "$TB_DIM" else _tb_center "$TB_ARCH $TOOLBOX_VERSION" "$TB_DIM" fi _tb_bot printf '\n' } _tb_main_screen() { _tb_banner _tb_head '请选择要执行的操作' _tb_pair '🌐 1. 网络服务' '💽 4. 重装系统' _tb_pair '⚡ 2. 安装 BBR' '🛠️ 5. 常用工具' _tb_pair '📦 3. 安装基础依赖' '🔄 99. 重新启动' _tb_pair '🩺 DIAG 只读体检' '🚪 0. 退出脚本' _tb_row '🔐 TLS 证书管理（本地开发）' _tb_bot if (( TB_WIDTH < 64 )); then printf '\n %s· 输入编号回车；A1 / B9 快捷可用%s\n' "$TB_DIM" "$TB_RESET" printf ' %s· 修改前会提示影响并确认%s\n\n' "$TB_DIM" "$TB_RESET" else printf '\n %s· 输入编号回车；完整编号 A1 / B9 也可直接输入%s\n' "$TB_DIM" "$TB_RESET" printf ' %s· 安装、修改和重启前会显示影响并二次确认%s\n\n' "$TB_DIM" "$TB_RESET" fi } _tb_network_screen() { _tb_head '🌐 网络服务' _tb_row '📡 1. Alpine 版 HY2' _tb_row '📡 2. Alpine 版 Xray' _tb_row '📡 3. TUIC 服务' _tb_row '📡 4. HY2 服务' _tb_row '📡 5. Xray 服务' _tb_sep _tb_row '↩️ 0. 返回主菜单' _tb_bot; printf '\n' printf ' %sAlpine 两项仅在 Alpine + OpenRC 可用。%s\n\n' "$TB_DIM" "$TB_RESET" } _tb_apps_screen() { _tb_head '🛠️ 常用工具' _tb_pair '🐳 1. Docker' '🧰 9. IT-Tools' _tb_pair '📊 2. NodeQuality' '🔐 10. WebSSH' _tb_pair '📁 3. Alist' '🧱 11. 1Panel' _tb_pair '🐱 4. 哪吒面板' '⚙️ 12. TCP Brutal' _tb_pair '🪪 5. X-UI' '🌍 13. MyIP' _tb_pair '💾 6. 虚拟内存' '🛰️ 14. 回程测试' _tb_pair '📈 7. 流量告警' '📨 15. TG 通知' _tb_pair '🔑 8. 修改密码' '🚀 16. 网络优化' _tb_sep _tb_pair '🧭 17. Sun-panel' '🏔️ 19. Alpine 工具' _tb_pair '📝 20. Memos' '🔀 21. NPM' _tb_pair '✍️ 22. Halo' '🌐 23. DNS 切换' _tb_pair '🧩 24. NginxWebUI' '🖥️ 25. Nexterm' _tb_pair '📺 26. LibreTV' '📄 27. Stirling PDF' _tb_pair '✅ 28. 网页验证' '🧬 29. DDNS-GO' _tb_pair '🔒 30. 密码管理器' '🔁 31. Relay 转发' _tb_sep _tb_pair '🕳️ 18. 预留' '↩️ 0. 返回主菜单' _tb_bot; printf '\n' } _tb_read_choice() { printf ' %s🎯%s 请输入数字选择操作：' "$TB_CYAN" "$TB_RESET" IFS= read -r TB_CHOICE || { printf '\n'; return 1; } TB_CHOICE=${TB_CHOICE^^} } _tb_pause() { if [[ -t 0 && -t 1 ]]; then local answer printf '\n %s↩ 操作结束，按回车返回当前菜单…%s' "$TB_DIM" "$TB_RESET" IFS= read -r answer || return 1 fi } _tb_bye() { printf '\n' _tb_top '╔' '╗' _tb_center '👋 感谢使用 Terry Toolbox' "$TB_BOLD$TB_CYAN" _tb_center ' ' _tb_center '期待下次见，留好备用登录方式。' "$TB_DIM" _tb_center ' ' _tb_bot printf '\n' } _tb_bootstrap_has() { command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; } _tb_bootstrap_exec() { "$@" &2; return 1; } missing=() for command in curl wget socat sudo nano openssl tar ss; do _tb_bootstrap_has "$command" || missing+=("$command"); done (( ${#missing[@]} == 0 )) || { printf ' %s仍缺少%s：%s\n' "$TB_RED" "$TB_RESET" "${missing[*]}" >&2; return 1; } printf ' %s✓%s 依赖检查通过。\n' "$TB_GREEN" "$TB_RESET" } # END bundled module: entry show_list() { printf '%s\n' 'A1 A2 A3 A4 A5' 'B1 B2 B3 B4 B5 B6 B7 B8 B9 B10 B11 B12 B13 B14 B15 B16 B17 B18 B19 B20 B21 B22 B23 B24 B25 B26 B27 B28 B29 B30 B31' } show_help() { printf 'Terry 工具箱 %s\n用法: bash toolbox.sh [--help|--list]\n--list 列出菜单编号；不带参数进入交互菜单。\n' "$TOOLBOX_VERSION" } # Bundled modules; load_modules is intentionally a no-op. TOOLBOX_BUNDLED=1 load_modules() { :; } # BEGIN bundled module: dependencies #!/usr/bin/env bash # Source-only dependency support. No package operation runs until a caller asks # for missing commands and the operator confirms. Compatible with Bash 3.2+. # Public: toolbox_require_commands 'feature' cmd...; toolbox_require_docker 'feature'; # toolbox_require_kernel_headers 'feature' running-release. # Return 0 only after command readback, 1 on refusal/failure, 2 for bad arguments. # Anchor lazy loading at source time; BASH_SOURCE may be a basename and the # caller may change directory before requesting Docker. Bundles have no peers. if [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} != 1 ]]; then _TOOLBOX_DEPS_MODULE_DIR=${BASH_SOURCE[0]%/*} [[ $_TOOLBOX_DEPS_MODULE_DIR != "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" ]] || _TOOLBOX_DEPS_MODULE_DIR=. _TOOLBOX_DEPS_MODULE_DIR=$(CDPATH= builtin cd -- "$_TOOLBOX_DEPS_MODULE_DIR" && builtin pwd -P) || return 1 fi _toolbox_deps_error() { printf '%s\n' "$*" >&2; } _toolbox_deps_has() { command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; } _toolbox_deps_root() { [[ ${EUID:-$(id -u)} == 0 ]] || { _toolbox_deps_error '补装依赖需要 root；未执行安装。'; return 1; } } _toolbox_deps_exec() { # Package managers must not consume the caller's next menu/feature answer. # stdout also belongs on stderr: callers may themselves capture stdout. "$@" &2 } _toolbox_deps_os() { local file=${TOOLBOX_OS_RELEASE:-/etc/os-release} line value id='' count=0 [[ -f $file && -r $file ]] || { _toolbox_deps_error "无法读取系统信息：${file}；未执行安装。"; return 1; } # Read metadata as data. Never source/eval os-release or infer an unrelated # distribution from ID_LIKE; package mappings below are explicitly scoped. while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n $line ]]; do [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*ID=(.*)$ ]] || continue value=${BASH_REMATCH[1]} case $value in \"*\"|\'*\') value=${value:1:${#value}-2};; esac id=$value count=$((count + 1)) done < "$file" if [[ $count == 1 ]]; then case $id in debian|ubuntu|alpine) printf '%s\n' "$id"; return 0;; esac fi _toolbox_deps_error '依赖自动补装仅支持 ID=debian、ubuntu 或 alpine；系统信息未知或不受支持，未执行安装。' return 1 } # Return 0 when the distribution CA package is installed, 1 when absent, and 2 # for an unreadable state. Do not inspect/repair CA files or change any caller # CA override: package installation only supplies the distribution's defaults. _toolbox_deps_ca_package() { local os=$1 status='' rc=0 case $os in debian|ubuntu) _toolbox_deps_has dpkg-query || { _toolbox_deps_error '缺少 dpkg-query，无法确认 ca-certificates 包状态；未继续。'; return 2; } status=$(dpkg-query -W -f='${Status}' -- ca-certificates /dev/null) || rc=$? case $rc:$status in '0:install ok installed') return 0;; 1:*|0:*' not-installed'|0:*' config-files') return 1;; esac;; alpine) _toolbox_deps_has apk || { _toolbox_deps_error '缺少 apk，无法确认 ca-certificates 包状态；未继续。'; return 2; } apk info --exists ca-certificates /dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$? case $rc in 0) return 0;; 1) return 1;; esac;; esac _toolbox_deps_error '无法可靠确认 ca-certificates 包状态；请检查包管理器，未继续。' return 2 } _toolbox_deps_package() { local os=$1 command=$2 package='' case $command in bash|curl|wget|socat|sudo|nano|openssl|python3|tar|unzip|zip|gzip|bzip2|cpio|dmidecode|virt-what|certbot|nginx|jq|lsof|strace|tcpdump|ethtool|iperf3|vnstat|make|dkms|efibootmgr|mokutil) package=$command;; awk) package=gawk;; grep) package=grep;; sed) package=sed;; find|xargs) package=findutils;; xz|unxz) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=xz; else package=xz-utils; fi;; gpg) package=gnupg;; gpgv) package=gpgv;; cc|gcc) package=gcc;; c++|g++) package=g++;; ar|as|ld|nm|objcopy|objdump|ranlib|readelf|strip|strings) package=binutils;; modprobe|lsmod|depmod|modinfo|insmod|rmmod) package=kmod;; cat|cp|mv|rm|mkdir|rmdir|env|id|uname|stat|readlink|realpath|sha256sum|sha512sum|md5sum|sha1sum|sort|head|tail|wc|cut|tr|tee|touch|chmod|chown|chgrp|install|mktemp|timeout|date|sleep|ln|dd|df|du|sync|truncate|base64|basename|dirname|seq|od|shred|nproc|whoami|cksum|comm|split|uniq|yes|chroot|mkfifo|mknod|nice|nohup|stty) package=coreutils;; ps|free|uptime|top|pgrep|pkill|pmap|pwdx|sysctl|vmstat|watch) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=procps-ng; else package=procps; fi;; killall|fuser|pstree) package=psmisc;; ip|ss|tc|bridge) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then case $command in ip) package=iproute2-minimal;; ss) package=iproute2-ss;; tc) package=iproute2-tc;; bridge) package=iproute2;; esac else package=iproute2; fi;; iptables|ip6tables|iptables-save|iptables-restore|ip6tables-save|ip6tables-restore) package=iptables;; nft) package=nftables;; netstat|ifconfig|route|arp) package=net-tools;; hostname|domainname|dnsdomainname) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=net-tools; else package=hostname; fi;; ping|ping6) package=iputils-ping;; tracepath|tracepath6) package=iputils-tracepath;; traceroute) package=traceroute;; mtr) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=mtr; else package=mtr-tiny; fi;; nc|netcat) package=netcat-openbsd;; dig|nslookup) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=bind-tools; else package=bind9-dnsutils; fi;; host) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=bind-tools; else package=bind9-host; fi;; ssh-keygen) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=openssh-keygen; else package=openssh-client; fi;; ssh|scp|sftp) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=openssh-client-default; else package=openssh-client; fi;; getent) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=musl-utils; else package=libc-bin; fi;; passwd) # Alpine's community/shadow owns /usr/bin/passwd; BusyBox remains # sufficient when its passwd applet is already available. # https://pkgs.alpinelinux.org/contents?file=passwd&path=%2Fusr%2Fbin&name=shadow&branch=v3.24&repo=community&arch=x86_64 if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=shadow; else package=passwd; fi;; tput) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=ncurses; else package=ncurses-bin; fi;; lsblk|findmnt|flock|blkid|lscpu|losetup|uuidgen|wipefs|fstrim|sfdisk|partx|mount|umount|dmesg|logger|hexdump) # Alpine splits these out of util-linux. Installing the meta-package # also pulls unrelated login/OpenRC pieces, so use only each provider. if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=$command else case $command in mount|umount|losetup) package=mount;; sfdisk) package=fdisk;; hexdump) package=bsdextrautils;; logger) package=bsdutils;; uuidgen) package=uuid-runtime;; *) package=util-linux;; esac fi;; fdisk|lsmem|mountpoint|mkswap|swapon|swapoff|fallocate|nsenter|unshare|setsid|getopt|ionice) if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then package=util-linux-misc else case $command in fdisk) package=fdisk;; swapon|swapoff) package=mount;; *) package=util-linux;; esac fi;; apt-cache|dpkg|dpkg-query|dpkg-deb) [[ $os != alpine ]] || return 1 case $command in apt-cache) package=apt;; *) package=dpkg;; esac;; *) return 1;; esac printf '%s\n' "$package" } _toolbox_deps_confirm() { local answer='' printf '%s [y/N]：' "$1" >&2 if ! IFS= read -r answer; then _toolbox_deps_error '未收到完整确认（EOF）；已取消补装。'; return 1 fi case $answer in y|Y|yes|YES) return 0;; esac _toolbox_deps_error '已取消补装；未执行安装。' return 1 } toolbox_require_commands() { if (( $# < 2 )) || [[ -z $1 ]]; then _toolbox_deps_error "用法：toolbox_require_commands '功能名' 命令..."; return 2 fi local feature=$1 command os manager package invalid=0 needs_ca=0 install_ca=0 ca_status=0 local missing=() packages=() remaining=() shift for command in "$@"; do [[ $command =~ ^[a-zA-Z0-9_][a-zA-Z0-9_.+-]*$ ]] || { _toolbox_deps_error "无效的依赖命令名：$command"; return 2; } _toolbox_deps_has "$command" && continue [[ " ${missing[*]-} " == *" $command "* ]] || missing+=("$command") done (( ${#missing[@]} > 0 )) || return 0 _toolbox_deps_error "$feature 缺少命令：${missing[*]}" # A missing product, init or bootloader is not permission to replace it. # Reject the whole batch before a prompt or any package-manager operation. for command in "${missing[@]}"; do case $command in docker) _toolbox_deps_error 'Docker 需要通过 B1 安装；请调用 toolbox_require_docker 或进入 B1 完成安装。'; invalid=1;; dockerd) _toolbox_deps_error '缺少 dockerd：Docker 安装不完整；已有 Docker/数据需要人工修复，不能通过普通依赖补装接管。'; invalid=1;; systemctl|systemd-*|journalctl|resolvectl|rc-service|rc-status|rc-update|openrc*|init|reboot|shutdown|grub*|update-grub*|extlinux|syslinux|linux-headers*) _toolbox_deps_error "$command 属于 init、引导器或内核环境，不能作为普通依赖自动安装或切换；请先确认本机现有环境。"; invalid=1;; apt-get|apk) _toolbox_deps_error "缺少系统包管理器 ${command}；无法自动补装包管理器。"; invalid=1;; esac done (( invalid == 0 )) || return 1 os=$(_toolbox_deps_os) || return 1 for command in "${missing[@]}"; do if ! package=$(_toolbox_deps_package "$os" "$command"); then _toolbox_deps_error "未定义 $os 上命令 $command 的软件包映射；未猜测包名，未执行安装。" invalid=1 continue fi [[ " ${packages[*]-} " == *" $package "* ]] || packages+=("$package") case $command in curl|wget|openssl|certbot) needs_ca=1;; esac done (( invalid == 0 )) || return 1 if (( needs_ca == 1 )); then _toolbox_deps_ca_package "$os" || ca_status=$? case $ca_status in 0) ;; 1) packages+=(ca-certificates); install_ca=1;; *) return 1;; esac fi case $os in debian|ubuntu) manager=apt-get;; alpine) manager=apk;; esac _toolbox_deps_error "对应软件包（${manager}）：${packages[*]}" _toolbox_deps_has "$manager" || { _toolbox_deps_error "未找到系统包管理器 ${manager}；未执行安装。"; return 1; } _toolbox_deps_root || return 1 _toolbox_deps_confirm '仅补装上述缺失依赖，成功后继续当前功能？' || return 1 case $manager in apt-get) if ! DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive _toolbox_deps_exec apt-get update -o APT::Update::Error-Mode=any; then _toolbox_deps_error 'apt-get update 失败；请检查网络、软件源或包管理锁；未继续安装。'; return 1 fi if ! DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive _toolbox_deps_exec apt-get install -y --no-upgrade --no-install-recommends -- "${packages[@]}"; then _toolbox_deps_error "apt-get install 失败：${packages[*]}；请检查上方错误，当前功能已停止。"; return 1 fi;; apk) # --no-cache fetches current indexes without changing repositories. if ! _toolbox_deps_exec apk add --no-cache -- "${packages[@]}"; then _toolbox_deps_error "apk add 失败：${packages[*]}；请检查网络、现有软件源或包管理锁，当前功能已停止。"; return 1 fi;; esac hash -r for command in "$@"; do _toolbox_deps_has "$command" && continue [[ " ${remaining[*]-} " == *" $command "* ]] || remaining+=("$command") done if (( ${#remaining[@]} > 0 )); then _toolbox_deps_error "安装后仍缺少命令：${remaining[*]}；命令回读失败，请检查包输出和 PATH，当前功能已停止。" return 1 fi if (( install_ca == 1 )) && ! _toolbox_deps_ca_package "$os"; then _toolbox_deps_error '安装后 ca-certificates 包状态回读失败；当前功能已停止。' return 1 fi _toolbox_deps_error "$feature 依赖补装完成，命令回读通过。" return 0 } # B12 needs headers for the running kernel, rather than another kernel image or # a generic headers meta-package. These two boundaries allow isolated tests. _toolbox_deps_kernel_release() { uname -r; } _toolbox_deps_kernel_build() { printf '/lib/modules/%s/build\n' "$1"; } toolbox_require_kernel_headers() { if (( $# != 2 )) || [[ -z $1 || ! $2 =~ ^[0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._+-]*$ ]]; then _toolbox_deps_error "用法：toolbox_require_kernel_headers '功能名' 当前内核版本"; return 2 fi local feature=$1 release=$2 running build os package running=$(_toolbox_deps_kernel_release) || { _toolbox_deps_error '无法读取正在运行的内核版本；未安装 headers。'; return 1; } [[ $release == "$running" ]] || { _toolbox_deps_error "请求的内核版本 $release 与正在运行的内核 $running 不一致；未安装 headers。"; return 1; } build=$(_toolbox_deps_kernel_build "$release") || return 1 [[ -d $build && -r $build/Makefile ]] && return 0 os=$(_toolbox_deps_os) || return 1 case $os in debian|ubuntu) ;; *) _toolbox_deps_error '当前功能仅支持 Debian/Ubuntu 的匹配内核 headers 补装；未安装或更换内核。'; return 1;; esac package=linux-headers-$release _toolbox_deps_error "$feature 缺少当前内核 $release 的编译 headers：$build" _toolbox_deps_error "对应软件包（apt-get）：$package" _toolbox_deps_has apt-get || { _toolbox_deps_error '未找到系统包管理器 apt-get；未安装 headers。'; return 1; } _toolbox_deps_root || return 1 _toolbox_deps_confirm '仅安装与正在运行内核完全匹配的 headers，成功后继续当前功能？' || return 1 if ! DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive _toolbox_deps_exec apt-get update -o APT::Update::Error-Mode=any; then _toolbox_deps_error 'apt-get update 失败；请检查网络、软件源或包管理锁；未继续安装 headers。'; return 1 fi if ! DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive _toolbox_deps_exec apt-get install -y --no-upgrade --no-install-recommends -- "$package"; then _toolbox_deps_error "apt-get install 失败：${package}；当前软件源可能没有此内核的 headers，当前功能已停止。"; return 1 fi if [[ ! -d $build || ! -r $build/Makefile ]]; then _toolbox_deps_error "headers 安装后编译目录回读失败：$build/Makefile；当前功能已停止。"; return 1 fi _toolbox_deps_error "${feature}：当前内核 headers 补装完成。" return 0 } toolbox_require_docker() { if (( $# != 1 )) || [[ -z $1 ]]; then _toolbox_deps_error "用法：toolbox_require_docker '功能名'"; return 2 fi _toolbox_deps_has docker && return 0 _toolbox_deps_error "$1 缺少 Docker；可先运行 B1 安装流程，完成后继续当前功能。" _toolbox_deps_root || return 1 _toolbox_deps_confirm '先安装 Docker？B1 仍会核对现有环境并要求原有安全确认' || return 1 local directory=${_TOOLBOX_DEPS_MODULE_DIR:-} if [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} == 1 ]] && { ! declare -F toolbox_network_detect_os >/dev/null 2>&1 || ! declare -F _toolbox_system_docker >/dev/null 2>&1; }; then _toolbox_deps_error '独立打包文件缺少 B1 函数，无法安装 Docker；请重新获取完整工具箱。'; return 1 fi if ! declare -F toolbox_network_detect_os >/dev/null 2>&1; then [[ -n $directory && -r $directory/network.sh ]] || { _toolbox_deps_error '缺少 network.sh，无法加载 B1 安装流程；请下载完整项目。'; return 1; } # shellcheck source=/dev/null source "$directory/network.sh" >&2 || return 1 fi if ! declare -F _toolbox_system_docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then [[ -r $directory/system.sh ]] || { _toolbox_deps_error '缺少 system.sh，无法加载 B1 安装流程；请下载完整项目。'; return 1; } # shellcheck source=/dev/null source "$directory/system.sh" >&2 || return 1 fi # Keep stdin attached for B1's own confirmations. B1's package-manager # boundaries protect stdin separately; never pipe synthetic approval to it. if ! _toolbox_system_docker >&2; then _toolbox_deps_error 'B1 Docker 安装未完成；当前功能已停止。'; return 1 fi hash -r _toolbox_deps_has docker || { _toolbox_deps_error 'B1 返回后仍缺少 docker；命令回读失败，当前功能已停止。'; return 1; } _toolbox_deps_error "$1：Docker 安装完成，继续当前功能。" return 0 } # END bundled module: dependencies # BEGIN bundled module: apps #!/usr/bin/env bash # Source this file; nothing runs until toolbox_app NUMBER is called. # Docker CLI and a working daemon must already be installed. All host ports are # loopback-only on Docker Engine >= 28. Each app gets its own bridge network; # this is not a firewall against host users, privileged containers, custom routing, # direct routing or other containers deliberately connected to that network. # No host networking or Docker socket mounts. Existing data requires explicit # reuse confirmation and may be modified by the app; nothing is deleted. # Only the default local Unix socket is accepted. A socket proxy could still point # to a remote daemon: the operator must ensure it really is the local Engine. # Multi-platform manifest digests resolved from official registries on 2026-10-30; # SHA-256 verified against manifest bytes and registry digest header. B28 is single-arch (amd64). # Pinning is not # signature verification, security review or a guarantee of application readiness. # Pins require deliberate security updates and compatibility testing before reuse. # Stirling PDF remains mutable: its registry returned HTTP 403 during validation. # References: upstream READMEs at github.com/{AlistGo/alist,CorentinTh/it-tools, # jason5ng32/MyIP,hslr-s/sun-panel,usememos/memos,NginxProxyManager/nginx-proxy-manager, # halo-dev/halo,cym1102/nginxWebUI,gnmyt/Nexterm,LibreSpark/LibreTV, # Stirling-Tools/Stirling-PDF,jeessy2/ddns-go,dani-garcia/vaultwarden}; # https://docs.nexterm.dev/installation # https://nezha.wiki/en_US/guide/dashboard.html # https://github.com/MHSanaei/3x-ui/wiki/Installation # # B28 来源证据: https://github.com/doubleDimple/mfa-start 的 Dockerfile（eclipse-temurin:8-jdk-jammy, # WORKDIR /mfa-start, EXPOSE 9999, H2 数据写 ./data）与该仓库自带 deploy.yaml 引用的 # lovele/mfa-start 完全一致；Docker Hub 账号 lovele 逐个 release 发布同名标签（2.2.4–2.2.52， # 最新标签与 release 同一分钟推送），但注册表元数据没有源码仓库标注，属高可信推断而非官方背书。 # 上游官方部署方式是下载未校验的 JAR + 脚本，故这里改用镜像 digest 固定。 # 应用只有表单登录，release 配置内置默认 admin/admin1234，可用 SPRING_SECURITY_USER_* 覆盖。 # 镜像仅提供 linux/amd64 单架构（非 manifest list）。 # B10 WebSSH 仍留在 modules/webssh.sh：上游只有 Dockerfile 与 compose 自建，没有官方镜像。 # A subshell prevents secrets, umask and local environment changes leaking to the caller. # Remember only the source directory; dependency work remains lazy. Bundles # already contain the core and must never look beside the downloaded script. _TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR='' if [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} != 1 ]]; then case ${BASH_SOURCE[0]} in */*) _TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR=${BASH_SOURCE[0]%/*};; *) _TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR=.;; esac _TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR=$(CDPATH= builtin cd -- "$_TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR" && builtin pwd -P) || _TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR='' fi # 以 HOME 属主的身份，独占创建 HOME 之下缺失的祖先目录（不含 HOME 本身）。 # 不做任何 chown/chmod：由本次 mkdir 建出的目录天然归执行身份；已存在的目录原样 # 跳过，符号链接或非目录拒绝；mkdir 因竞态失败也按同样规则处理，绝不接管既有对象。 # HOME 属主为 root（真 root 或 sudo -i）时直接由 root 创建，模式 0700；普通用户 0755。 _toolbox_apps_make_parents() { local home=${1%/} target=$2 rel acc part owner uid gid mode local -a parts=() [[ -n $home ]] || home=/ [[ $target == "$home"/* ]] || { printf '数据目录不在 HOME 之下：%s\n' "$target" >&2; return 1; } [[ -d $home && ! -L $home ]] || { printf 'HOME 不是普通目录：%s\n' "$home" >&2; return 1; } owner=$(stat -c '%u:%g' -- "$home") || { printf '无法读取 HOME 属主。\n' >&2; return 1; } uid=${owner%%:*}; gid=${owner##*:} if [[ $uid == 0 ]]; then mode=0700 else mode=0755 if ! command -v setpriv >/dev/null 2>&1; then declare -F toolbox_require_commands >/dev/null || { [[ -n ${_TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR:-} ]] && source "$_TOOLBOX_APPS_MODULE_DIR/dependencies.sh"; } || return 1 toolbox_require_commands '应用数据目录' setpriv || return 1 fi fi rel=${target#"$home"/} IFS=/ read -r -a parts <<< "$rel" acc=$home for part in "${parts[@]}"; do [[ -n $part && $part != . && $part != .. ]] || { printf '数据目录路径非法：%s\n' "$target" >&2; return 1; } acc=$acc/$part if [[ -L $acc ]]; then printf '%s 是符号链接，拒绝接管；请人工处理。\n' "$acc" >&2; return 1; fi if [[ -d $acc ]]; then continue; fi if [[ -e $acc ]]; then printf '%s 已存在且不是目录，拒绝接管。\n' "$acc" >&2; return 1; fi if [[ $uid == 0 ]]; then mkdir -m "$mode" -- "$acc" 2>/dev/null else setpriv --reuid="$uid" --regid="$gid" --clear-groups -- mkdir -m "$mode" -- "$acc" 2>/dev/null fi || { # 竞态：别人先放了东西。已是目录就原样保留并继续；其余一律拒绝。 if [[ -L $acc ]]; then printf '%s 被换成符号链接，拒绝接管。\n' "$acc" >&2; return 1; fi [[ -d $acc ]] || { printf '无法创建 %s（HOME 属主 uid %s 无权限或路径被占用）。\n' "$acc" "$uid" >&2; return 1; } } done } toolbox_app() ( local id=${1-} name image default_port target_port data_target='' note='' port answer local dependency root data names secret extra_ports='' environment='' context endpoint version major network_id reuse=0 canonical subdir local -a args=() if [[ $# != 1 ]];
