count legitimately change during this transaction. out.append([current, s.st_dev, s.st_ino, stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode), s.st_uid, s.st_gid]) return out class Leaf: def __init__(self, path): self.path = path self.parent = os.path.dirname(path) self.name = os.path.basename(path) self.chain = pathchain(self.parent) self.fd = os.open(self.parent, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW) need([os.fstat(self.fd).st_dev, os.fstat(self.fd).st_ino] == self.chain[-1][1:3], '父路径竞争') def close(self): os.close(self.fd) def stable(self): need(pathchain(self.parent) == self.chain, '父路径已改变；保留证据') def read(self): self.stable() try: fd = os.open(self.name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=self.fd) except FileNotFoundError: return dict(exists=False), b'' try: s = os.fstat(fd) need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid == 0 and not s.st_mode & 0o022 and s.st_nlink == 1, '配置/记录不是可信单链接普通文件: '+self.path) need(s.st_size <= 2*1024*1024, '配置/记录过大') with os.fdopen(os.dup(fd), 'rb') as f: data = f.read(2*1024*1024+1) t = os.fstat(fd) need(attrs(s) == attrs(t) and len(data) == s.st_size, '文件读取竞争') return dict(exists=True, **attrs(s), sha256=sha(data)), data finally: os.close(fd) def replace(self, expected, data, mode=0o600, uid=0, gid=0): self.stable() need(self.read()[0] == expected, '配置/inode/内容已改变；拒绝覆盖') name = '.'+self.name+'.b1-'+uuid.uuid4().hex fd = os.open(name, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600, dir_fd=self.fd) try: with os.fdopen(os.dup(fd), 'wb') as f: f.write(data); f.flush(); os.fsync(f.fileno()) os.fchown(fd, uid, gid); os.fchmod(fd, mode); os.fsync(fd) self.stable() need(self.read()[0] == expected, '发布前配置竞争；拒绝覆盖') if expected['exists']: os.replace(name, self.name, src_dir_fd=self.fd, dst_dir_fd=self.fd) else: os.link(name, self.name, src_dir_fd=self.fd, dst_dir_fd=self.fd, follow_symlinks=False) os.unlink(name, dir_fd=self.fd) os.fsync(self.fd) result = self.read()[0] need(result['sha256'] == sha(data) and result['mode'] == mode and result['uid'] == uid and result['gid'] == gid, '发布 readback 失败') return result finally: os.close(fd) try: os.unlink(name, dir_fd=self.fd) except FileNotFoundError: pass def remove(self, expected): need(self.read()[0] == expected, '删除前 owned 配置改变；保留') self.stable(); os.unlink(self.name, dir_fd=self.fd); os.fsync(self.fd) need(not self.read()[0]['exists'], '删除 readback 失败') return dict(exists=False) def readleaf(path): l = Leaf(path) try: return l.read() finally: l.close() def lock_check(): # A flock only protects its inode. Replacing the name would let a second # controller acquire a different lock, so re-prove fd/name/parent identity # immediately before configuration or service mutations, including rollback. need(LOCK is not None, '事务独占锁未持有') leaf, fd, expected = LOCK leaf.stable() actual=attrs(os.fstat(fd)) need(actual == {k:expected[k] for k in actual} and leaf.read()[0] == expected, '锁 inode/内容已改变；拒绝变更并保留证据') def config_snapshot(): parent = os.path.dirname(C['config']) chain = pathchain(parent, missing=True) if not os.path.exists(parent): need(chain and chain[-1][0] == os.path.dirname(parent), '仅允许创建缺失的 /etc/docker 叶目录') return dict(exists=False), b'', chain s, data = readleaf(C['config']) return s, data, chain def os_identity(): path = C['os'] # Native Alpine and systemd distros can ship this exact standard symlink. if os.path.islink(path): target = os.readlink(path) need(not TEST and target in ('../usr/lib/os-release', '/usr/lib/os-release'), 'os-release symlink 不可信') path = '/usr/lib/os-release' s, data = readleaf(path) need(s['exists'], '缺少 os-release') vals = {} for line in data.decode().splitlines(): if re.fullmatch(r'ID=[A-Za-z0-9_"\'-]+', line): vals['ID'] = line.split('=', 1)[1].strip('"\'') distro = vals.get('ID') need(distro in ('debian', 'ubuntu', 'alpine'), '仅支持 Debian/Ubuntu 或 Alpine') if TEST: init = readleaf(TEST+'/init')[1].decode().strip() else: init = pathlib.Path('/proc/1/comm').read_text().strip() if distro == 'alpine': need(init in ('init', 'openrc-init', 'openrc'), 'Alpine 必须是 booted OpenRC') pathchain(C['openrc'], openrc=True) need(os.path.isfile(C['openrc']+'/softlevel'), 'OpenRC 未引导；拒绝 chroot/未 booted 环境') level = pathlib.Path(C['openrc']+'/softlevel').read_text().strip() need(level not in ('', 'sysinit', 'single', 'shutdown'), 'OpenRC runlevel 不安全') return dict(distro=distro, init='openrc', os=s) need(init == 'systemd', 'Debian/Ubuntu 必须由 systemd 引导') return dict(distro=distro, init='systemd', os=s) def commands(init): for cmd in ('docker', 'dockerd', 'ip', 'ss', 'iptables', 'ip6tables', 'systemctl' if init == 'systemd' else 'rc-service'): p = shutil.which(cmd) need(p, '依赖预检后仍缺少命令 '+cmd+'；请检查安装结果；不会切换 init 或接管既有 Docker') p = os.path.realpath(p) pathchain(os.path.dirname(p)) s = os.stat(p) need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid == 0 and not s.st_mode & 0o022, '命令来源/权限不可信: '+cmd) CMDS[cmd] = p def run(cmd, *args, codes=(0,)): p = subprocess.Popen([CMDS.get(cmd, cmd), *args], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE) try: out, err = p.communicate(timeout=25) except BaseException: p.kill(); p.wait(); raise if LOG: if os.path.lexists(C['state']): need(pathchain(C['state'])==M['state_parents'], '事务证据父路径已改变') log_fd=os.open(LOG,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_APPEND|os.O_NOFOLLOW) try: s=os.fstat(log_fd) need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid==0 and stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)==0o600 and s.st_nlink==1, '操作证据权限/链接不可信') with os.fdopen(os.dup(log_fd),'ab') as f: # Configuration contents and daemon credentials stay private. f.write(encode(dict(command=cmd, args=list(args), rc=p.returncode, stdout=out.decode(errors='replace'), stderr=err.decode(errors='replace')))) f.flush(); os.fsync(f.fileno()) finally: os.close(log_fd) need(p.returncode in codes, '命令失败: '+cmd+' '+(' '.join(args[:2]))) return out.decode().strip(), p.returncode def docker(*args): return run('docker', '--host', 'unix:///var/run/docker.sock', *args)[0] def local_target(): need(not any(k.startswith('DOCKER_') for k in os.environ), 'DOCKER_* 环境造成 context/remote 歧义；请清除') need(run('docker', 'context', 'show')[0] == 'default', 'remote/context 已拒绝：只支持 default') need(run('docker', 'context', 'inspect', 'default', '--format', '{{.Endpoints.docker.Host}}')[0] == 'unix:///var/run/docker.sock', 'remote endpoint 已拒绝：必须默认 local Unix socket') version = docker('version', '--format', '{{.Server.Version}}') m = re.fullmatch(r'([0-9]{1,3})\.([0-9]+)\.([0-9]+)(?:\+[A-Za-z0-9.+_-]+)?', version) need(m and int(m[1]) >= 28, '需要 stable Engine >=28；prerelease/未知版本已拒绝') info = parse(docker('info', '--format', '{{json .}}')) need(isinstance(info, dict) and isinstance(info.get('ID'), str) and info['ID'], 'Engine ID 读取失败') need(info.get('Swarm', {}).get('LocalNodeState') == 'inactive', 'Swarm daemon 不支持安全重启') need(isinstance(info.get('SecurityOptions'), list) and not any('rootless' in str(x) for x in info['SecurityOptions']), 'rootless daemon 不支持') return dict(version=version, id=info['ID']) def no_containers(): text = docker('container', 'ls', '--no-trunc', '--format', '{{.ID}}') need(not text, '有运行 container/容器或读取不明；拒绝重启以避免业务中断') def socket_pids(): text = run('ss', '-H', '-xlpn')[0] rows = [x for x in text.splitlines() if re.search(r'(?:^|\s)/(?:var/)?run/docker\.sock(?:\s|$)', x)] result = [] for row in rows: need('LISTEN' in row, 'socket 状态不明') ps = re.findall(r'pid=(\d+)', row) need(ps, '默认 socket 无可核验 PID') result.extend(int(x) for x in ps) return set(result) def executable(pid): need(isinstance(pid, int) and pid > 1, 'dockerd PID 无效') proc = '/proc/'+str(pid) exe = os.readlink(proc+'/exe') need(exe == CMDS['dockerd'] and not exe.endswith(' (deleted)'), 'dockerd actual executable 不匹配') status = pathlib.Path(proc+'/status').read_text() uid = re.search(r'^Uid:\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)', status, re.M) need(uid and all(int(x) == 0 for x in uid.groups()), 'dockerd 不是 root daemon') raw = pathlib.Path(proc+'/cmdline').read_bytes() need(raw.endswith(b'\0'), 'dockerd argv 不完整') argv = [x.decode() for x in raw[:-1].split(b'\0')] need(argv and os.path.realpath(argv[0]) == exe, 'dockerd argv/executable 不一致') # bind PID to its start time, preventing stale PID-only ownership assumptions. start = pathlib.Path(proc+'/stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()[19] l = Leaf(exe) fd = os.open(l.name, os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=l.fd) try: before = os.fstat(fd) need(stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) and before.st_uid==0 and not before.st_mode & 0o022 and before.st_nlink==1, 'dockerd binary 权限不可信') h=hashlib.sha256() with os.fdopen(os.dup(fd),'rb') as f: for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''): h.update(chunk) need(attrs(os.fstat(fd))==attrs(before), 'dockerd executable 读取竞争') l.stable() s=dict(exists=True, **attrs(before), sha256=h.hexdigest()) finally: os.close(fd); l.close() return dict(pid=pid, start=start, exe=s, argv=argv) def systemd_properties(unit): text = run('systemctl', 'show', unit, '--no-pager', '--all', '--property=LoadState,ActiveState,SubState,MainPID,FragmentPath,DropInPaths,Job')[0] vals = {} for row in text.splitlines(): need('=' in row, unit+' readback 无效') k, v = row.split('=', 1) need(k not in vals, unit+' duplicate property') vals[k] = v required=('LoadState','ActiveState','SubState','FragmentPath','DropInPaths','Job') if unit.endswith('.service'): required+=('MainPID',) need(all(k in vals for k in required), unit+' readback 缺字段') return vals def service(init, running=True, before_stop=False, probe=False): if init == 'systemd': vals = systemd_properties('docker.service') need(vals['LoadState'] == 'loaded' and vals['Job'] == '' and vals['DropInPaths'] == '', 'systemd 未 loaded/pending job/drop-in 冲突') fragment = vals['FragmentPath'] need(fragment.endswith('/docker.service') or TEST and fragment == TEST+'/unit', 'docker.service FragmentPath 不可信') if not TEST: fragment = os.path.realpath(fragment) need(fragment in ('/usr/lib/systemd/system/docker.service','/etc/systemd/system/docker.service'), 'custom systemd fragment 不支持') snap, data = readleaf(fragment) need(snap['exists'], 'systemd fragment 缺失') need(re.fullmatch(r'\d+', vals['MainPID']), 'systemd MainPID 无效') active = vals['ActiveState'] == 'active' and vals['SubState'] == 'running' socket_fragment = None # The upstream unit uses socket activation. PID 1 also owns its fd; # rollback must quiesce that exact socket unit before stopped readback. activation = active and b'fd://' in pathlib.Path('/proc/'+vals['MainPID']+'/cmdline').read_bytes() activation = activation or not active and M is not None and 'socket' in M['init']['resources'] if activation: sv=systemd_properties('docker.socket') socket_active=sv.get('ActiveState')=='active' and sv.get('SubState') in ('listening','running') socket_stopped=sv.get('ActiveState')=='inactive' and sv.get('SubState')=='dead' need(sv.get('LoadState')=='loaded' and sv.get('DropInPaths')=='' and sv.get('Job')=='' and (socket_active if active else socket_stopped or before_stop and socket_active), 'docker.socket pending/状态/drop-in 冲突') socket_fragment=sv.get('FragmentPath','') if not TEST: socket_fragment=os.path.realpath(socket_fragment) need(socket_fragment in ('/usr/lib/systemd/system/docker.socket','/etc/systemd/system/docker.socket'), 'custom docker.socket 不支持') socket_snap,socket_data=readleaf(socket_fragment) need(socket_snap['exists'],'docker.socket fragment 缺失') streams=re.findall(rb'^ListenStream=(.*)$',socket_data,re.M) need(streams in ([b'/run/docker.sock'],[b'/var/run/docker.sock']), 'docker.socket 非默认 Unix listener') stopped = vals['ActiveState'] == 'inactive' and vals['SubState'] == 'dead' and vals['MainPID'] == '0' need(active if running else active or stopped, 'systemd 状态不是明确 running/stopped') need(re.fullmatch(r'\d+', vals['MainPID']), 'systemd MainPID 无效') pid = int(vals['MainPID']) resources = dict(fragment=fragment, fragment_snapshot=snap) if socket_fragment: resources.update(socket=socket_fragment, socket_snapshot=socket_snap) else: resolved = run('rc-service', '--resolve', 'docker')[0] need(resolved == C['script'], 'OpenRC 服务 resolve 不是确切 docker script') script, data = readleaf(C['script']); conf, unused = readleaf(C['conf']) need(script['exists'], 'OpenRC init script 缺失') text, rc = run('rc-service', 'docker', 'status', codes=(0,3,4,8,16,32)) active = rc == 0 and re.search(r'\bstatus:\s+started\b', text) is not None stopped = rc == 3 and re.search(r'\bstatus:\s+stopped\b', text) is not None need(active if running else active or stopped, 'OpenRC status/退出码不是明确 started/stopped') pid = 0 if active: # Modern Alpine uses supervise-daemon without dockerd.pid. Correlate # the local listener to a native root executable; pidfile is optional # additional evidence, never an assumed distro invariant. listeners=socket_pids() need(len(listeners)==1,'OpenRC 默认 socket listener PID 不唯一') pid=next(iter(listeners)) ps, raw = readleaf(C['pid']) if ps['exists']: need(re.fullmatch(rb'[0-9]+\n?', raw) and int(raw)==pid, 'OpenRC docker pidfile/listener 不一致') resources = dict(script=script, conf=conf, resolved=resolved) result = dict(resources=resources, active=active, pid=pid) if active: result['process'] = executable(pid) need(pid in socket_pids(), 'local socket listener 不属于实际 dockerd PID；代理已拒绝') need(socket_pids() <= ({pid,1} if 'socket' in resources else {pid}), 'local socket 存在未知 proxy PID') else: listeners=socket_pids() # `probe` is the stopped preflight read: the caller decides whether a # leftover listener (socket activation pending) is acceptable. need(not listeners or probe or before_stop and 'socket' in resources and listeners=={1}, 'stopped 后 socket 仍有未知 listener；保留证据') return result def flags(argv, config): names = [] kept = [] aliases = dict(H='hosts', host='hosts', D='debug', b='bridge', p='pidfile', s='storage-driver', G='group', **{'registry-mirror':'registry-mirrors', 'default-address-pool':'default-address-pools', 'label':'labels', 'feature':'features'}) i = 1 while i < len(argv): a = argv[i]; need(a.startswith('-'), 'dockerd flags 含未知位置参数') key, equal, value = a.lstrip('-').partition('=') if len(key) > 1 and key[0] in aliases and not a.startswith('--'): value = key[1:]; key = key[0]; equal = '=' key = aliases.get(key, key) end = i+1 if not equal and end < len(argv) and not argv[end].startswith('-'): value = argv[end]; end += 1 need(key not in ('ipv6','fixed-cidr-v6','ip6tables','bip6','default-gateway-v6','experimental','rootless'), 'dockerd flags 冲突: '+key) need(key not in ('bridge',), 'custom bridge flags 不支持') if key in ('iptables','ip-forward','ip-masq','icc'): need(value in ('','true'), '安全 flags 已禁用: '+key) if key == 'firewall-backend': need(value == 'iptables', 'firewall-backend flags 未验收') if key == 'config-file': need(value == '/etc/docker/daemon.json', '非默认 config-file flags 已拒绝') else: need(key not in config, 'JSON/flags duplicate 配置: '+key) if key == 'hosts': need(value in ('unix:///var/run/docker.sock','unix:///run/docker.sock','fd://'), 'remote dockerd host flags 不支持') names.append(key); kept.extend(argv[i:end]) i = end need(config.get('hosts', ['unix:///var/run/docker.sock']) in (['unix:///var/run/docker.sock'], ['unix:///run/docker.sock']), 'remote JSON hosts 已拒绝') need(config.get('iptables', True) is True and config.get('ip6tables', True) is True, 'iptables/ip6tables 安全禁用冲突；不会接管') need(config.get('experimental', False) is False, 'experimental 现有配置不支持；不会开启') need(config.get('firewall-backend','iptables') == 'iptables', '仅实现 iptables 后端，nftables 未验收') # Same safety switches the flag parser rejects must be gated in JSON too. for key in ('ip-forward', 'ip-masq', 'icc'): need(config.get(key, True) is True, 'JSON 安全开关已禁用: '+key) need('bridge' not in config and 'bip6' not in config and 'default-gateway-v6' not in config, 'custom bridge/IPv6 gateway 配置不支持') return kept def ula(value): try: net = ipaddress.IPv6Network(value, strict=True) except ValueError: raise Refusal('IPv6 必须为无 host bits 的 ULA /64') need(net.prefixlen == 64 and net.subnet_of(ipaddress.IPv6Network('fd00::/8')), 'IPv6 必须为 fd00::/8 locally-assigned ULA /64') return str(net) def topology(subnet, owned=False): ids = docker('network', 'ls', '--no-trunc', '--format', '{{.ID}}').splitlines() need(ids and len(ids) == len(set(ids)) and all(re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{64}', x) for x in ids), 'Docker network 列表不完整/无效') networks = parse(docker('network', 'inspect', *ids)) need(isinstance(networks, list) and len(networks) == len(ids) and {x.get('Id') for x in networks if isinstance(x,dict)} == set(ids), 'network readback 不完整') net = ipaddress.IPv6Network(subnet) bridge = None for n in networks: need(isinstance(n.get('IPAM'), dict), 'network IPAM 无效') configs = n['IPAM'].get('Config') if n.get('Driver') in ('host','null') and n.get('Name') in ('host','none'): need(configs in (None,[]), '内置 host/none 意外 IPAM 配置') continue need(isinstance(configs,list), 'network IPAM Config 无效') isbridge = n.get('Name') == 'bridge' and n.get('Driver') == 'bridge' if isbridge: need(bridge is None, '多个 default bridge'); bridge = n for c in n['IPAM']['Config']: need(isinstance(c,dict) and isinstance(c.get('Subnet'),str), 'network subnet 无效') other = ipaddress.ip_network(c['Subnet']) if other.version == 6 and net.overlaps(other): need(owned and isbridge and str(other) == subnet and n.get('EnableIPv6') is True, 'IPv6 network 子网冲突') need(bridge is not None, '缺少 default bridge') if not owned: need(bridge.get('EnableIPv6') is False, '既有 bridge IPv6 不接管') routes = parse(run('ip', '-j', '-6', 'route', 'show', 'table', 'all')[0]) addrs = parse(run('ip', '-j', '-6', 'addr', 'show')[0]) need(isinstance(routes,list) and isinstance(addrs,list), 'IPv6 路由/地址读取不完整') for row in routes: need(isinstance(row,dict) and isinstance(row.get('dst'),str), 'IPv6 route 数据无效') # RA expiry is a kernel countdown, not route identity. Keep its # presence in the fingerprint but not its naturally changing value; # every probe still checks the actual destination for overlap below. if 'expires' in row: need(type(row['expires']) is int and row['expires'] >= 0, 'IPv6 route expires 无效') row['expires'] = None dst = row['dst'] if dst in ('default','::/0'): continue other = ipaddress.IPv6Network(dst, strict=False) need(not net.overlaps(other) or owned and row.get('dev') == 'docker0', 'IPv6 route 路由冲突') for row in addrs: need(isinstance(row,dict) and isinstance(row.get('addr_info'),list), 'IPv6 addr 数据无效') for a in row['addr_info']: if a.get('family') != 'inet6': continue other = ipaddress.IPv6Network(str(a.get('local'))+'/'+str(a.get('prefixlen')), strict=False) need(not net.overlaps(other) or owned and row.get('ifname') == 'docker0', 'IPv6 host 地址冲突') for key in ('valid_life_time','preferred_life_time'): if key in a: need(type(a[key]) is int and a[key] >= 0, 'IPv6 地址 lifetime 无效') a[key] = None return dict(networks=networks, routes=routes, addrs=addrs) def preflight(subnet, owned=False): need(os.geteuid() == 0, 'apply/restore 需要 root') identity = os_identity(); commands(identity['init']) need(pathlib.Path(C['disable']).read_text().strip() == '0', '内核 IPv6 不可用；本模块不写 sysctl') # Service state is read WITHOUT any Docker API call. The upstream unit is # socket-activated, so `docker version|info` on a stopped daemon would START # it via docker.socket and silently break the "does not start" contract. svc = service(identity['init'], running=False, probe=True) if not svc['active']: need(not socket_pids(), 'docker 服务已停但默认 socket 仍在监听；调用 Docker API 会激活 daemon' ' 启动（不承接启动语义）；请先自行启动 docker') need(False, 'docker 服务未运行（socket 也未监听）；本模块不启动服务，请先自行启动 docker') # Proven running + listener owned by this exact dockerd: now the API is safe. engine = local_target(); no_containers() snap, data, chain = config_snapshot() config = parse(data) if snap['exists'] else {} need(isinstance(config,dict), 'daemon JSON 顶层必须为 object') if not owned: need('ipv6' not in config and 'fixed-cidr-v6' not in config, '既有 ipv6/fixed-cidr-v6 不接管') validateflags = flags(svc['process']['argv'], config) return dict(identity=identity, engine=engine, service=svc, snapshot=snap, original=data, parents=chain, config=config, flags=validateflags, topology=topology(subnet, owned)) def stable_identity(p): return dict(identity=p['identity'], engine=p['engine'], resources=p['service']['resources'], exe=p['service']['process']['exe'], flags=p['flags']) def confirm(action, subnet, p): print('B1 Docker IPv6 本地模块：Debian/Ubuntu systemd、booted Alpine OpenRC 尚未真实验收。') print('操作: '+action+'；配置: '+C['config']) print('仅变更 default bridge 的 ipv6=true / fixed-cidr-v6='+subnet+' / ip6tables=true；保留无关 JSON，不开启 experimental。' if action == 'apply' else '恢复原始配置字节/权限，原本不存在则仅删除 precise owned daemon.json。') print('服务: '+p['identity']['init']+' docker；将重启现有 dockerd，不安装/升级/启动未运行服务。') print('dockerd actual executable: '+CMDS['dockerd']+'；PID '+str(p['service']['pid'])) print('validate: '+shlex.join([CMDS['dockerd'],'--validate','--config-file=<私有候选>']) +'；保留 '+str(len(p['flags']))+' 个已核验实际 argv token（不展示可能含凭据的参数）。') print('JSON 精确预览：只展示本模块触及的三个键；无关原始字节及权限备份在私有记录。') if action=='apply': print(json.dumps({'ipv6':True,'fixed-cidr-v6':subnet,'ip6tables':True},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)) else: m_preview,unused=load_record() old=parse(base64.b64decode(m_preview['original_b64'],validate=True)) if m_preview['original']['exists'] else {} print(json.dumps({k:old[k] for k in ('ipv6','fixed-cidr-v6','ip6tables') if k in old},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)) print('网络影响: default bridge 地址与 IPv6 firewall/端口发布规则会变化；服务可能中断；无运行容器才允许。') print('ULA 不提供公网 IPv6；不创建/删除容器或网络，本模块不写 sysctl（重启后 dockerd 会自行设置' ' IPv6 forwarding 与 FORWARD 规则），不宣称公网或容器通信通过。') print('私有原始备份/事务证据: '+C['state']+'；失败保守保留，未知改变不覆盖。') for n in (1,2): print('完整换行输入 YES 确认 ('+str(n)+'/2): ', end='', flush=True) line = sys.stdin.readline() need(line.endswith('\n') and line[:-1].lower() == 'yes', '取消：必须完整换行双 YES；EOF 取消') print() def mkdir_safe(path): parent = pathchain(os.path.dirname(path)) fd = os.open(os.path.dirname(path), os.O_RDONLY|os.O_DIRECTORY|os.O_NOFOLLOW) try: need(pathchain(os.path.dirname(path)) == parent, 'mkdir 父路径竞争') os.mkdir(os.path.basename(path), 0o700, dir_fd=fd); os.fsync(fd) finally: os.close(fd) return pathchain(path) def record(): global MSNAP leaf = Leaf(C['state']+'/record.json') try: MSNAP = leaf.replace(MSNAP or dict(exists=False), encode(M)) finally: leaf.close() def load_record(): chain = pathchain(C['state']) need(chain[-1][3] == 0o700, '初始化记录目录权限不可信') s, raw = readleaf(C['state']+'/record.json') need(s['exists'] and s['mode'] == 0o600, '初始化记录缺失/权限不可信') m = parse(raw) need(isinstance(m,dict) and m.get('schema') == 1 and m.get('config_path') == C['config'] and m.get('state_parents') == chain and m.get('phase') in ('prepared','published','applied','restore-published','restored','rolled-back','retained'), '初始化记录结构不可信') original = base64.b64decode(m['original_b64'], validate=True) need(m['original_sha256'] == sha(original), '原始备份内容不可信') old = parse(original) if m['original']['exists'] else {} need(isinstance(old,dict) and 'ipv6' not in old and 'fixed-cidr-v6' not in old, '原始配置不是模块初始化状态') if m['original']['exists']: need(m['original']['sha256'] == sha(original) and m['original']['uid'] == 0 and m['original']['nlink'] == 1 and not m['original']['mode'] & 0o022, '原始权限/inode 记录不可信') else: need(original == b'', '不存在的原始配置不能有内容') expected = dict(old, ipv6=True, **{'fixed-cidr-v6':ula(m['subnet']), 'ip6tables':True}) need(m['candidate_sha256'] == sha(encode(expected)), '候选配置初始化摘要不可信') owned = m.get('owned') need(isinstance(owned,dict), 'owned 记录无效') if owned: need(owned.get('sha256') == m['candidate_sha256'] and owned.get('nlink') == 1, 'precise owned 配置记录不可信') else: need(m['phase'] in ('prepared','retained') and m.get('current') == m['original'], '缺少 precise owned 配置记录') if m['phase'] == 'applied': need(m.get('current') == owned and m.get('config_parents') == pathchain(os.path.dirname(C['config'])), 'owned 父路径/当前记录已改变') return m, s def validate(data, p): path = C['state']+'/validate-'+uuid.uuid4().hex+'.json' l = Leaf(path) try: snap = l.replace(dict(exists=False), data) run(CMDS['dockerd'], '--validate', '--config-file='+path, *p['flags']) need(l.read()[0] == snap, 'validate 候选内容改变') finally: l.close() def resources_match(m, svc): need(svc['resources'] == m['init']['resources'], 'init/service 资源已改变；拒绝操作') if svc['active']: need(svc['process']['exe'] == m['init']['exe'], 'dockerd executable 已改变；拒绝操作') def verify_runtime(p, svc, phase): """Full post-(re)start recheck. /proc argv is re-read here on purpose: service()/flags() re-prove the default Unix listener and the executable identity, and any flag injected by a changed unit or wrapper (e.g. --host=tcp://...) must fail, not pass on a stale pre-restart snapshot. """ lock_check() need(svc['active'], phase+' 后服务不是 running') resources_match(M, svc) snap, data, unused = config_snapshot() need(snap == M['current'], phase+' 后配置 readback 不匹配') try: again = flags(svc['process']['argv'], parse(data) if snap['exists'] else {}) except Refusal as e: raise Refusal(phase+' 后 dockerd actual argv/flags 复验失败: '+str(e)) need(again == p['flags'], phase+' 后 dockerd actual argv/flags 已改变（默认 Unix list/目标身份复验）') need(svc['pid'] in socket_pids() and socket_pids() <= {svc['pid'], 1}, phase+' 后默认 Unix socket listener 不属于实际 dockerd') need(local_target() == p['engine'], phase+' 后 Engine ID/version 改变') def restart_and_verify(p, target): global ATTEMPTED no_containers() resources_match(M, service(p['identity']['init'])) need(config_snapshot()[0] == M['current'], 'restart 前 precise owned 配置已改变') lock_check() ATTEMPTED = True if p['identity']['init'] == 'systemd': run('systemctl', 'restart', 'docker.service') else: run('rc-service', 'docker', 'restart') verify_runtime(p, service(p['identity']['init']), 'restart') no_containers() bridge = parse(docker('network', 'inspect', 'bridge')) need(isinstance(bridge,list) and len(bridge)==1 and bridge[0].get('Name')=='bridge' and bridge[0].get('Driver')=='bridge', 'bridge readback 无效') b = bridge[0] if target == 'apply': need(b.get('EnableIPv6') is True and any(x.get('Subnet') == M['subnet'] for x in b.get('IPAM',{}).get('Config',[])), 'bridge IPv6/subnet readback 失败') else: need(b.get('EnableIPv6') is False, '原始 bridge readback 未恢复') lock_check() def rollback(p, previous, data, phase): # Never stop or restore when either the exact target or trusted init changed. global ATTEMPTED lock_check() need(config_snapshot()[0] == M['current'], 'rollback：未知配置改变；不 stop、不覆盖') svc = service(p['identity']['init'], running=False, before_stop=True); resources_match(M, svc) if svc['active']: no_containers() validate(data if previous['exists'] else b'{}\n', p) need(config_snapshot()[0] == M['current'], 'rollback validate 后未知配置改变') lock_check() if ATTEMPTED: if p['identity']['init'] == 'systemd': targets=['docker.service']+(['docker.socket'] if 'socket' in M['init']['resources'] else []) run('systemctl', 'stop', *targets) else: run('rc-service', 'docker', 'stop') stopped = service(p['identity']['init'], running=False); resources_match(M, stopped) need(not stopped['active'], 'rollback stop readback 未 stopped；保留') leaf = Leaf(C['config']) try: lock_check() if previous['exists']: M['current'] = leaf.replace(M['current'], data, previous['mode'], previous['uid'], previous['gid']) else: M['current'] = leaf.remove(M['current']) finally: leaf.close() record() if ATTEMPTED: # Original daemon was running; successful stop is a prerequisite to start. lock_check() if p['identity']['init'] == 'systemd': run('systemctl', 'start', 'docker.service') else: run('rc-service', 'docker', 'start') verify_runtime(p, service(p['identity']['init']), 'rollback start') no_containers() bridge = parse(docker('network', 'inspect', 'bridge')) expect = phase == 'applied' need(isinstance(bridge,list) and len(bridge)==1 and bridge[0].get('EnableIPv6') is expect, 'rollback bridge readback 失败') lock_check() M['phase'] = phase if phase == 'applied': M['owned'] = M['current'] M['rollback'] = dict(configuration=True, service_readback=ATTEMPTED, target_phase=phase) record() def transaction(action, subnet, p): global M, MSNAP, LOG, PUBLISHED before = p['snapshot']; olddata = p['original'] lock_check() if action == 'apply': need(not os.path.lexists(C['state']), '已有事务/证据目录；不会接管或覆盖，请人工检查') parents = mkdir_safe(C['state']) LOG = C['state']+'/operations.log' with open(LOG, 'xb') as f: os.chmod(LOG,0o600) candidate = encode(dict(p['config'], ipv6=True, **{'fixed-cidr-v6':subnet, 'ip6tables':True})) M = dict(schema=1, phase='prepared', config_path=C['config'], state_parents=parents, original=before, original_b64=base64.b64encode(olddata).decode(), original_sha256=sha(olddata), original_parents=p['parents'], init=stable_identity(p), subnet=subnet, candidate_sha256=sha(candidate), current=before, owned={}) record() else: M, MSNAP = load_record() LOG = C['state']+'/operations.log' s, unused = readleaf(LOG); need(s['exists'] and s['mode']==0o600, '操作证据不可信') need(M['phase'] == 'applied' and before == M['owned'] and stable_identity(p) == M['init'], 'restore 必须当前 precise owned 配置及可信 init；拒绝未知改变') candidate = base64.b64decode(M['original_b64'],validate=True) try: validate(candidate if candidate else b'{}\n', p) # This full recheck catches service/config/topology/container races inside lock. check = preflight(subnet, owned=action=='restore') need(check == p, '锁内 validate 后复检发现 race/配置/网络/服务改变') lock_check() parent = os.path.dirname(C['config']) if not os.path.exists(parent): M['created_config_parent'] = mkdir_safe(parent) record() leaf = Leaf(C['config']) try: lock_check() if action=='restore' and not M['original']['exists']: M['current'] = leaf.remove(before) else: meta = before if action=='apply' else M['original'] M['current'] = leaf.replace(before, candidate, meta.get('mode',0o644), meta.get('uid',0), meta.get('gid',0)) finally: leaf.close() PUBLISHED = True M['config_parents'] = pathchain(parent) M['phase'] = 'published' if action=='apply' else 'restore-published' if action=='apply': M['owned'] = M['current'] record() restart_and_verify(p, action) M['phase'] = 'applied' if action=='apply' else 'restored'; record() print('B1 '+action+' 完成：配置、service/Engine/default bridge 已回读；不代表真实 IPv6 容器通信。') except BaseException as e: if PUBLISHED: try: rollback(p, before, olddata, 'rolled-back' if action=='apply' else 'applied') print('操作失败；已回滚并回读，私有证据保留。', file=sys.stderr) except BaseException as rb: M['phase'] = 'retained'; M['failure'] = str(e); M['rollback_failure'] = str(rb) try: record() except BaseException: pass print('rollback 保守保留：'+str(rb)+'；证据 '+C['state'], file=sys.stderr) else: M['failure'] = str(e) try: record() except BaseException: pass raise def status(): print('B1 Docker IPv6 本地模块；Debian/Ubuntu systemd、booted Alpine OpenRC 尚未真实验收。') snap, data, unused = config_snapshot() if snap['exists']: config = parse(data); need(isinstance(config,dict), 'daemon JSON 顶层无效') print('配置: '+json.dumps({k:config[k] for k in ('ipv6','fixed-cidr-v6','ip6tables') if k in config},ensure_ascii=False)) else: print('未配置：daemon.json 不存在。') if os.path.lexists(C['state']): m, unused = load_record() print('事务阶段: '+m['phase']+'；证据: '+C['state']) if m['phase']=='applied': need(snap==m['owned'], 'precise owned 配置已改变；不能 restore') else: print('无模块初始化记录；既有 IPv6 不接管。') print('只读；不联网、不写文件、不查询 Docker、不启动 daemon。') def main(): global LOCK if TEST: need(os.path.normpath(TEST)==TEST and TEST.startswith('/') and re.fullmatch(r'docker-ipv6-[A-Za-z0-9_-]+', os.path.basename(TEST)), '测试路径只允许 private scratch docker-ipv6-*') chain = pathchain(TEST) need(chain[-1][3] == 0o700, '测试 root 必须 root-owned 0700') need(not any(k.startswith('TOOLBOX_DOCKER_IPV6_') and k!='TOOLBOX_DOCKER_IPV6_TEST_ROOT' for k in os.environ), '拒绝额外路径/命令覆盖；仅支持 private scratch TEST_ROOT') args = sys.argv[1:] need(args and args[0] in ('apply','status','restore') and len(args) in ((1,2) if args[0]=='apply' else (1,)), '用法: toolbox_docker_ipv6 apply [ULA_/64] | status | restore') if args[0]=='status': status(); return action=args[0] if action=='restore': m, unused = load_record(); need(m['phase']=='applied', 'restore：初始化记录不是 applied；保留证据') subnet=ula(m['subnet']) else: subnet=ula(args[1] if len(args)==2 else 'fd00:1234::/64') p=preflight(subnet, owned=action=='restore') if action=='apply': need(not os.path.lexists(C['state']), '已有事务证据，不接管') else: need(p['snapshot']==m['owned'] and stable_identity(p)==m['init'], 'restore precise owned/init 已改变') confirm(action,subnet,p) lock=Leaf(C['lock']) try: lock.stable() fd=os.open(lock.name, os.O_RDWR|os.O_CREAT|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_NONBLOCK,0o600,dir_fd=lock.fd) try: s=os.fstat(fd) need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid==0 and stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)==0o600 and s.st_nlink==1, '独占锁不可信') try: fcntl.flock(fd,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB) except BlockingIOError: raise Refusal('concurrent：另一事务持有独占锁') lock.stable() expected=lock.read()[0] need(expected['dev']==s.st_dev and expected['ino']==s.st_ino, '锁 inode 已改变') LOCK=(lock,fd,expected) lock_check() need(preflight(subnet, owned=action=='restore')==p, '锁内复检发现 race；未变更') transaction(action,subnet,p) finally: LOCK=None os.close(fd) finally: lock.close() def interrupt(signum, frame): raise Refusal('收到 signal '+str(signum)+'；取消并保守回滚') signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,interrupt) signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,interrupt) try: main() except (Refusal, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e: print('B1 拒绝/失败: '+str(e),file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(1) PY } # END bundled module: docker-ipv6 # BEGIN bundled module: webssh-containers #!/usr/bin/env bash # B10 two ORIGINAL container products; source-only, does not change Python WebSSH. # Provenance: registry-1.docker.io raw manifests + Docker-Content-Digest verified # 2026-10-31. The pins are immutable OCI indexes; explicitly select linux/amd64 # and verify native image/RepoDigests readback. The other index entries may be # attestations or other platforms and do not imply arm64 support here. # personal: github.com/jinhuaitao/WebSSH, OCI revision # 1fda43b09371fa24b4faf4ffc968d10529439f3f. Native cookie login + 2FA exists; # native config is seeded with local random credentials before first start. # Upstream saveData uses data.json.tmp + rename: a single data.json bind mount # is WRONG (EBUSY). Mount a private directory and use /data as working directory; # original /app/webssh-app stays intact. 077 umask avoids upstream 0644 defaults. # easynode: github.com/chaos-zhu/easynode; pinned app layer package version 3.8.4, # app/db.js prints initial credentials, app/config/index.js stores DB + logs in # /easynode/app/db. Retain them privately; never print Docker logs/inspect secrets. # guacd is required by upstream guacamole-lite RDP service and has no host port. # Missing ordinary tools need confirmation; no hostnet, socket mount, watchtower # or image mirrors. # Loopback and bridge are not a firewall against host users/privileged routing. # Pins are byte reproducibility, not signature verification or a security audit. # Remember only the source directory; dependency work remains lazy. Bundles # already contain the core and must never look beside the downloaded script. _TOOLBOX_WEBSSH_CONTAINERS_MODULE_DIR='' if [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} != 1 ]]; then case ${BASH_SOURCE[0]} in */*) _TOOLBOX_WEBSSH_CONTAINERS_MODULE_DIR=${BASH_SOURCE[0]%/*};; *) _TOOLBOX_WEBSSH_CONTAINERS_MODULE_DIR=.;; esac _TOOLBOX_WEBSSH_CONTAINERS_MODULE_DIR=$(CDPATH= builtin cd -- "$_TOOLBOX_WEBSSH_CONTAINERS_MODULE_DIR" && builtin pwd -P) || _TOOLBOX_WEBSSH_CONTAINERS_MODULE_DIR='' fi wsc_error() { printf 'B10: %s\n' "$*" >&2; return 1; } wsc_docker() { # flock protects an inode, not its pathname. Re-prove the held descriptor # before every Docker mutation, also when a signal/failure invokes cleanup. case ${1-}:${2-} in pull:*|create:*|start:*|stop:*|rm:*|network:create|network:rm) [[ -n ${lockfd-} ]] && wsc_python lock-check "$branch" "$HOME" || return 1 ;; esac docker --host unix:///var/run/docker.sock "$@" } # Embedded stdlib helper keeps the distributed one-file toolbox source-only. # No ledger is sourced as shell. Seal detects accidental/untrusted edits; a root # adversary controlling this private directory and key is outside this boundary. wsc_python() { python3 - "${lockfd-}" "$BASHPID" "$@" <<'PY' import hashlib,hmac,json,os,pathlib,re,secrets,shutil,stat,sys lockfd,lockpid,act,branch,home=sys.argv[1:6]; args=sys.argv[6:] root=pathlib.Path(home)/'.local/share/terry-toolbox/webssh-containers'/branch hexid=re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{64}$') label='org.terry.toolbox.wsc.' def fail(msg): raise ValueError(msg) def secure(p,directory=False): s=p.lstat() if stat.S_ISLNK(s.st_mode) or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid():fail('外来属主/符号链接: '+str(p)) if directory: if not stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode) or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)!=0o700:fail('目录须为私有0700: '+str(p)) elif not stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) or s.st_nlink!=1 or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)!=0o600:fail('文件须为独立0600: '+str(p)) return [s.st_dev,s.st_ino,stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode),s.st_uid] def paths(): if not home.startswith('/') or any(c in home for c in ',\n\r\x00') or str(pathlib.Path(home))!=home:fail('HOME路径非规范或含Docker参数分隔符') if str(pathlib.Path(home).resolve())!=home:fail('HOME/父路径含符号链接') p=pathlib.Path(home) if not p.is_dir() or p.stat().st_uid!=os.geteuid():fail('HOME不是当前用户真实目录') for part in ('.local','share','terry-toolbox','webssh-containers',branch): p=p/part if p.is_symlink():fail('数据父路径含符号链接') if p.exists(): s=p.stat() if not p.is_dir() or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)&0o022:fail('外来/可被他人写入的数据父目录') def write_new(p,b): fd=os.open(p,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600) with os.fdopen(fd,'wb') as f:f.write(b);f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno()) def atomic(p,b): secure(root,True) if p.exists():secure(p) t=root/('.'+p.name+'.'+secrets.token_hex(12));write_new(t,b);os.replace(t,p) def snapshot(p): s=p.lstat() if not stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode) or stat.S_ISLNK(s.st_mode) or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)&0o022:fail('目录父inode/权限不安全') return [s.st_dev,s.st_ino,stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode),s.st_uid] def body(d):return json.dumps(d,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')).encode() def seal(d): k=(root/'seal.key').read_bytes();b=body(d) atomic(root/'ledger.json',b);atomic(root/'ledger.mac',hmac.digest(k,b,'sha256').hex().encode()) def load(): paths();secure(root,True) for n in ('seal.key','ledger.json','ledger.mac','lock'):secure(root/n) b=(root/'ledger.json').read_bytes();k=(root/'seal.key').read_bytes() if len(k)!=32 or not hmac.compare_digest((root/'ledger.mac').read_text(),hmac.digest(k,b,'sha256').hex()):fail('ownership ledger封印损坏，保留全部资源') d=json.loads(b) if d.get('schema')!=1 or d.get('branch')!=branch or d.get('home')!=home or not hexid.fullmatch(d.get('token','')):fail('ownership ledger内容无效') for p,s in d['paths'].items(): if snapshot(pathlib.Path(p))!=s:fail('目录inode/权限/属主已改变，拒绝接管') for p,rec in d['files'].items(): f=pathlib.Path(p) if secure(f)!=rec['stat'] or hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=rec['hash']:fail('私有凭据/lock inode或内容已改变') if lockfd: if not lockfd.isdecimal() or not lockpid.isdecimal():fail('持有锁描述符无效') held=os.stat('/proc/'+lockpid+'/fd/'+lockfd) expected=d['files'][str(root/'lock')]['stat'] if [held.st_dev,held.st_ino,stat.S_IMODE(held.st_mode),held.st_uid]!=expected:fail('持有锁fd与ledger inode不同，拒绝变更') return d def read_one(file): j=json.loads(pathlib.Path(file).read_text()) if not isinstance(j,list) or len(j)!=1:fail('Docker返回不完整inspect') return j[0] def critical(c): # Engine 29 reports OomKillDisable null after start but false at create inspect; # normalize this boolean so the snapshot compare is not a false ownership change. hc=dict(c['HostConfig']); hc['OomKillDisable']=bool(hc.get('OomKillDisable')) return {'Id':c['Id'],'Name':c['Name'],'Image':c['Image'],'Config':c['Config'],'HostConfig':hc,'Mounts':c['Mounts']} def network(n):return {k:n[k] for k in ('Id','Name','Driver','Scope','Internal','Attachable','Ingress','Options','Labels')} def owned_net(d,n): if network(n)!=d['network']['critical']:fail('network ID/labels/driver已改变') peers=set(n.get('Containers',{}));known={c['id'] for c in d['containers'] if not c.get('removed')} if peers-known:fail('独立bridge有未知外来容器，拒绝停止/删除') def validate_intent(d,c,role,ref,iid): if not hexid.fullmatch(c.get('Id','')):fail('container ID无效') expected_name='terry-webssh-'+branch+('' if role=='app' else '-guacd') cfg=c['Config'];hc=c['HostConfig'];labels=cfg.get('Labels',{}) if c['Name']!='/'+expected_name or cfg['Image']!=ref or c['Image']!=iid or labels.get(label+'token')!=d['token'] or labels.get(label+'branch')!=branch or labels.get(label+'role')!=role:fail('create readback ownership/image不匹配') if hc['NetworkMode']!=d['network']['id'] or hc.get('Privileged') or hc.get('CapAdd') or hc.get('CapDrop')!=['ALL'] or hc.get('SecurityOpt')!=['no-new-privileges'] or not hc.get('ReadonlyRootfs') or hc.get('Binds') or hc.get('RestartPolicy',{}).get('Name')!='unless-stopped':fail('create readback权限/网络不匹配') target='8080/tcp' if branch=='personal' else '8082/tcp' ports={} if role=='guacd' else {target:[{'HostIp':'127.0.0.1','HostPort':str(d['port'])}]} if (hc.get('PortBindings') or {})!=ports:fail('create readback端口暴露不匹配') expected=[] if role=='guacd' else Missing distro dependencies use the shared consent flow. # No ACME implementation, firewall or DNS edits. # One exclusive native Certbot lineage per owned root. Defaults use the source package, # never a downloaded one-liner. Runtime/version hashes describe local bytes, not a # claim that the host's package repository was independently authenticated. # Overrides are isolated paths for fixtures; TEST_FIXTURE is a sealed, root-owned # fixture.json under /root/.../tls-acme-fixture-* and permits only loopback HTTPS CA. # Sourcing records the module directory and defines functions; it never installs. # Remember only the source directory; dependency work remains lazy. Bundles # already contain the core and must never look beside the downloaded script. _TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR='' if [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} != 1 ]]; then case ${BASH_SOURCE[0]} in */*) _TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR=${BASH_SOURCE[0]%/*};; *) _TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR=.;; esac _TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR=$(CDPATH= builtin cd -- "$_TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR" && builtin pwd -P) || _TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR='' fi tls_acme_err() { printf '%s\n' "$*" >&2; } tls_acme_root() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_ROOT:-/var/lib/terry-toolbox-acme}"; } tls_acme_units() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_UNIT_DIR:-/etc/systemd/system}"; } tls_acme_valid() { [[ ${#1} -le 253 && $1 =~ ^([a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63}$ ]] || return 1 [[ ${#2} -le 254 && $2 =~ ^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._+-]*@([a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63}$ ]] } tls_acme_confirm() { local answer i for i in 1 2; do printf '第 %s 次确认：输入 YES（完整换行；空白/EOF 取消）：' "$i" IFS= read -r answer && [[ ${answer^^} == YES ]] || { tls_acme_err '已取消。'; return 1; } done } tls_acme_dependencies() { local dependency for dependency in "$@"; do if ! command -v "$dependency" >/dev/null 2>&1; then declare -F toolbox_require_commands >/dev/null || { [[ -n ${_TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR:-} ]] && source "$_TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_MODULE_DIR/dependencies.sh"; } || return 1 toolbox_require_commands 'TLS 证书管理' "$@" || return 1 return 0 fi done } tls_acme_require() { local id='' line [[ ${EUID:-1} == 0 ]] || { tls_acme_err '需要 root。'; return 1; } tls_acme_dependencies python3 openssl certbot systemctl ss timeout || return 1 while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n $line ]]; do case $line in ID=*) [[ -z $id ]] || return 1; id=${line#ID=}; id=${id#\"}; id=${id%\"}; id=${id#\'}; id=${id%\'};; esac done < "${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_OS_RELEASE:-/etc/os-release}" || return 1 [[ $id == debian || $id == ubuntu ]] || { tls_acme_err '仅支持 Debian/Ubuntu + 真正 systemd。'; return 1; } [[ -d ${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_SYSTEMD_RUN:-/run/systemd/system} ]] || { tls_acme_err 'systemd 未运行。'; return 1; } # A marker directory alone is insufficient: real PID1 is checked unless the # explicitly sealed fixture permits a mock service manager. tls_acme_fs platform "${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_OS_RELEASE:-/etc/os-release}" "${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_SYSTEMD_RUN:-/run/systemd/system}" || return 1 tls_acme_version=$(certbot --version 2>/dev/null) || { tls_acme_err 'Certbot --version 失败。'; return 1; } [[ $tls_acme_version =~ ^certbot\ [0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]] || { tls_acme_err '无法识别稳定 Certbot 1 } # Filesystem helper: sealed ownership, restrictive ancestry, native symlinks only, # no eval/source of metadata or renewal hooks, no recursive cleanup on any failure. tls_acme_fs() { python3 - "$1" "$(tls_acme_root)" "$(tls_acme_units)" "${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_TEST_FIXTURE:-}" "${@:2}" <<'PY' import os, sys, stat, json, hashlib, re, socket, ssl, ipaddress, configparser, subprocess, tempfile, ValueError('incomplete archive generation') def certcheck(m, existing=False): renewal(m); h=m['host']; live=root+'/config/live/'+h # Existing sealed pairs may have expired while the host was offline. Their # ownership, chain signatures, purpose, hostname and key still gate recovery; # newly issued/deployed pairs always require the normal time checks too. def cmd(a): r=subprocess.run(['openssl']+a,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,timeout=15) if r.returncode: raise ValueError('certificate time/trust/hostname/keypair check failed') return r.stdout cert=os.path.realpath(live+'/cert.pem'); key=os.path.realpath(live+'/privkey.pem'); chain=os.path.realpath(live+'/chain.pem'); full=os.path.realpath(live+'/fullchain.pem') _,cb=record(cert,private=False); _,ch=record(chain,private=False); _,fb=record(full,private=False); record(key) if fb!=cb+ch: raise ValueError('fullchain is not exact cert+chain') if cb.count(b'-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----')!=1 or not ch.count(b'-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----'): raise ValueError('invalid leaf/intermediate chain') a=['verify','-purpose','sslserver','-verify_hostname',h] if existing: a+=['-no_check_time'] if F: a+=['-CAfile',F['cert_ca'],'-no-CApath'] # OpenSSL 1.1 has no CAstore. start=cmd(['x509','-in',cert,'-startdate','-noout']).decode().strip() if not start.startswith('notBefore=') or ssl.cert_time_to_seconds(start[10:])>time.time(): raise ValueError('owned certificate has not become valid') else: cmd(['x509','-in',cert,'-checkend','3600','-noout']) pub=cmd(['x509','-in',cert,'-pubkey','-noout']) kp=cmd(['pkey','-in',key,'-passin','pass:','-pubout']) if pub!=kp: raise ValueError('certificate/private key mismatch') # DNS SAN must be exactly this lineage: no wildcard/other service names. san=cmd(['x509','-in',cert,'-noout','-ext','subjectAltName']).decode() names=re.findall(r'DNS:([^,\s]+)',san) if names!=[h] or 'IP Address:' in san: raise ValueError('SAN differs from owned hostname') return full,key def unit_conflicts(m=None): paths={units,'/etc/systemd/system','/run/systemd/system','/usr/local/lib/systemd/system','/usr/lib/systemd/system','/lib/systemd/system'} expected=m['units'] if m else {} for u in paths: if not os.path.isdir(u): continue for n in (SVC,TIMER): p=u+'/'+n if os.path.lexists(p) and p not in expected: raise ValueError('foreign systemd unit') for n in is not None if dep.name in (SVC,TIMER) or (dep.is_symlink() and os.path.realpath(dep.path) in targets): if not owned: raise ValueError('foreign systemd dependency link') for p,r in expected.items(): ident(os.fstat(fd))!=m['link']['parent']: raise ValueError('timer wants parent changed') finally: os.close(fd) try: if op=='platform': for p in args: path(p) if not (F and F['mock_systemd']): if open('/proc/1/comm').read().strip()!='systemd': raise ValueError('PID1 is not systemd') elif op=='program': p=args[0]; rr,_=record(p,private=False) if not os.access(p,os.X_OK): raise ValueError('Certbot is not executable') elif op=='preflight': parent_ids() if os.path.lexists(root): raise ValueError('root exists; never adopt foreign/partial state') unit_conflicts() elif op=='fixture': if F: from urllib.parse import urlsplit u=urlsplit(F['server']) addresses={a[4][0] for a in socket.getaddrinfo(u.hostname,u.port,type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)} if not addresses or any(not socket.getaddrinfo(h,80,type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)}) if not addresses or any(not ipaddress.ip_address(a).is_global for a in addresses): raise ValueError('hostname must resolve to public A/AAAA; DNS ValueError('fixture mismatch') save(m) elif op=='binding': m=verify() if m['program']['path']!=args[0] or m['certbot_version']!=args[1]: raise ValueError('Certbot path/version differs from sealed bytes supplied via argument instead. data=args[0].encode() create(root+'/runtime.sh',data); save(m) elif op=='begin': m=verify() if args[0]=='renew': if m['state']!='ready': raise ValueError('not ready; retain failed attempt for manual review') renewal(m) m['state']='renewing' if args[0]=='renew' else 'pending'; save(m) elif op=='finish': # Only under the exclusive caller lock, after sys.exit(1) PY } tls_acme_port() { local out out=$(ss -ltnH 2>/dev/null) || { tls_acme_err 'ss 读取失败；端口 80 状态未知，拒绝。'; return 1; } python3 - "$out" <<'PY' import sys for line in sys.argv[1].splitlines(): a=line.split() if len(a)<5 or a[0]!='LISTEN' or ':' not in a[3]: print('ss 输出格式未知，拒绝。',file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(1) p=a[3].rsplit(':',1)[1] if not p.isdigit(): sys.exit(1) if p=='80': print('TCP 80 已有 listener；不停止别人的服务。',file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(1) PY } tls_acme_info() { local out out=$(tls_acme_fs verify "${1:-}") || return 1 mapfile -t tls_acme_meta <<< "$out" [[ ${#tls_acme_meta[@]} == 5 ]] || return 1 tls_acme_host=${tls_acme_meta[0]}; tls_acme_email=${tls_acme_meta[1]} } tls_acme_cleanup() { local rc=$? trap - EXIT INT TERM HUP if [[ -n ${tls_acme_lock_id:-} ]]; then tls_acme_fs unlock "$tls_acme_lock_id" || rc=1 fi exit "$rc" } tls_acme_lock_check() { [[ -n ${tls_acme_lock_id:-} ]] || return 1 tls_acme_fs lock-check "$tls_acme_lock_id" } tls_acme_runtime() { local text f local -a funcs=() # Freeze only this adapter's functions. VMess integration is loaded from the # verified source package snapshot when automatic sync is explicitly authorized. while IFS= read -r f; do case $f in tls_acme_*|toolbox_tls_acme) funcs+=("$f");; esac; done < <(compgen -A function) text=$(declare -f "${funcs[@]}") || return 1 text=$'#!/bin/bash\nset -u\n'"$text"$'\nTOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_ROOT='"$(tls_acme_root)"$'\nTOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_UNIT_DIR='"$(tls_acme_units)"$'\nTOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_TEST_FIXTURE='"${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_TEST_FIXTURE:-}"$'\nTOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_OS_RELEASE='"${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_OS_RELEASE:-/etc/os-release}"$'\nTOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_SYSTEMD_RUN='"${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_SYSTEMD_RUN:-/run/systemd/system}"$'\nif [[ ${BASH_SOURCE[0]} == "$0" ]]; then toolbox_tls_acme "$@"; fi\n' tls_acme_fs runtime "$text" } tls_acme_call() { local mode=$1 root log out server bundle tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_fs binding "$tls_acme_binary" "$tls_acme_version" && tls_acme_port || return 1 root=$(tls_acme_root) || return 1 out=$(tls_acme_fs fixture) || return 1 mapfile -t tls_acme_ca <<< "$out" server=${tls_acme_ca[0]}; bundle=${tls_acme_ca[1]:-} log=$(tls_acme_fs log) || return 1 tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_fs begin "$mode" || return 1 tls_acme_err "Certbot 私有日志：$log（失败保留，不输出账户/私钥）。" local -a argv=(--config "$root/cli.ini" --config-dir "$root/config" --work-dir "$root/work" --logs-dir "$root/logs" --server "$server" --non-interactive --no-directory-hooks --standalone --preferred-challenges http --http-01-port 80 --cert-name "$tls_acme_host") if [[ $mode == issue ]]; then argv=(certonly "${argv[@]}" --agree-tos --email "$tls_acme_email" -d "$tls_acme_host") else argv=(renew "${argv[@]}" --no-random-sleep-on-renew) if [[ ${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_TEST_FORCE_RENEWAL:-0} == 1 ]]; then [[ -n ${TOOLBOX_TLS_ACME_TEST_FIXTURE:-} ]] && tls_acme_fs binding "$tls_acme_binary" "$tls_acme_version" || { tls_acme_err 'force renewal 只允许 sealed fixture。'; return 1; } argv+=(--force-renewal) fi fi # No --no-verify-ssl/insecure fallback. return 1; } tls_acme_fs cert >/dev/null || return 1 } tls_acme_issue() ( local tls_acme_lock_id='' tls_acme_version='' tls_acme_binary='' tls_acme_host=${1:-} tls_acme_email=${2:-} [[ $# -le 2 ]] || return 2 tls_acme_require || return 1 if [[ -z $tls_acme_host ]]; trap 'exit 143' TERM HUP tls_acme_runtime || return 1 tls_acme_call issue || return 1 tls_acme_err 'Certbot 申请成功且本地 trust/time/hostname/keypair 与自有 native lineage 已验证。自动续期尚未授权。' ) tls_acme_renew() ( local background=$1 host=${2:-} tls_acme_lock_id='' tls_acme_version='' tls_acme_binary='' tls_acme_host='' tls_acme_email='' tls_acme_require && tls_acme_info "$host" || return 1 [[ ${tls_acme_meta[2]} == ready ]] || { tls_acme_err '上次操作未完成，需检查私有日志；拒绝自动重试。'; then tls_acme_deploy_internal authorized || return 1; fi tls_acme_err 'Certbot renew 成功，本地证书与归属 readback OK（未到期时 Certbot 可不 rotation）。' ) # Exact systemd snapshots. Unknown states/jobs/drop-ins never mean success. tls_acme_timer_read() { local expected=$1 n out line k v active load sub fragment drops job pid timer_running=0 service_owned=0 for n in terry-toolbox-acme.timer terry-toolbox-acme.service; do out=$(systemctl show "$n" --all -p LoadState -p ActiveState -p SubState -p FragmentPath -p DropInPaths -p Job -p MainPID 2>/dev/null) || return 1 active='' load='' sub='' fragment='' drops='' job='' pid=''; local -A seen=() while IFS= read -r line; do k=${line%%=*}; v=${line#*=}; [[ $line == *=* && -z ${seen[$k]:-} ]] || return 1; seen[$k]=1 case $k in LoadState) load=$v;; ActiveState) active=$v;; SubState) sub=$v;; FragmentPath) fragment=$v;; DropInPaths) drops=$v;; Job) job=$v;; MainPID) pid=$v;; *) return 1;; esac done <<< "$out" # systemd omits MainPID for timer units and never-loaded units; a loaded # service always reports it. Only those two shapes may lack it. [[ ${#seen[@]} == 7 && $pid =~ ^[0-9]+$ || ${#seen[@]} == 6 && -z $pid && ( $load == not-found || $n == *.timer ) ]] || return 1 [[ -z $drops ]] || { tls_acme_err '发现非受控 systemd drop-in；拒绝。'; return 1; } if [[ $expected == background && $n == *.service && $active == activating && $sub == start ]]; then [[ $pid == "$$" ]] || { tls_acme_err '续期服务 MainPID 不属于本 owned runtime；拒绝。'; return 1; } [[ -z $job || $job =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*(\ /org/freedesktop/systemd1/job/[1-9][0-9]*)?$ ]] || return 1 service_owned=1 else [[ -z $job && ( $pid == 0 || -z $pid ) ]] || return 1 fi if [[ $expected == absent ]]; then [[ $load == not-found && $active == inactive && $sub == dead && -z $fragment ]] || return 1 else [[ $load == loaded && $fragment == "$(tls_acme_units)/$n" ]] || return 1 if [[ $n == *.timer && ( $expected == active || $expected == background ) ]]; then [[ $active == active ]] || return 1 if [[ $expected == background && $sub == running ]]; then timer_running=1 else [[ $sub == waiting ]] || return 1; fi elif [[ $n == *.service && $expected == background ]]; then [[ ( $active == activating && $sub == start ) || ( $active == inactive && $sub == dead ) ]] || return 1 else [[ $active == inactive && $sub == dead ]] || return 1 fi fi done [[ $timer_running == 0 || $service_owned == 1 ]] } tls_acme_timer() ( local action=$1 host=${2:-} tls_acme_lock_id='' tls_acme_version='' tls_acme_binary='' tls_acme_host='' tls_acme_email='' tls_acme_require && tls_acme_info "$host" || return 1 [[ ${tls_acme_meta[2]} == ready ]] || return 1 if [[ $action == enable-renewal ]]; then tls_acme_fs cert >/dev/null || return 1 if [[ ${tls_acme_meta[3]} == yes ]]; then tls_acme_timer_read active && tls_acme_err '自动续期已授权且 timer readback OK。'; return; fi # No units on first enable; retained owned units after disable are supported. if [[ -e $(tls_acme_units)/terry-toolbox-acme.timer ]]; then tls_acme_timer_read inactive || return 1; else tls_acme_timer_read absent || return 1; fi tls_acme_err '预览：预授权每天 03/15 点 + 随机延迟自动 standalone HTTP-01；80 忙即失败，不停其他服务；只 owned unit/runtime/link。VMess 仅另行 deploy-vmess 授权后同步。' else tls_acme_fs cert --existing >/dev/null || return 1 tls_acme_timer_read active || { tls_acme_err 'timer/service 未处于明确受控静止/等待状态；保留。'; return 1; } tls_acme_err '预览：撤销自动续期授权；仅停止 owned timer，并移除精确 owned wants link；保留证书/日志/unit。' fi tls_acme_confirm || return 1 tls_acme_lock_id=$(tls_acme_fs lock) || return 1 trap tls_acme_cleanup EXIT trap 'exit 130' INT; trap 'exit 143' TERM HUP tls_acme_info "$host" || return 1 if [[ $action == enable-renewal ]]; then tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_fs authorize || return 1 tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 systemctl daemon-reload && tls_acme_lock_check && systemctl start terry-toolbox-acme.timer || { tls_acme_err '开启失败；保留 owned unit/link/授权现场，不宣称成功。'; return 1; } tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_info "$host" && tls_acme_timer_read active || { tls_acme_err 'timer readback 失败，保留现场。'; return 1; } else tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_timer_read active && tls_acme_fs unauthorize || return 1 tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 systemctl stop terry-toolbox-acme.timer || { tls_acme_err '停止失败；保留 link/unit（授权已撤销）。'; return 1; } tls_acme_timer_read inactive || { tls_acme_err '停止 readback 未确认；保留 link/unit。'; return 1; } tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_fs unlink && systemctl daemon-reload && tls_acme_info "$host" && tls_acme_timer_read inactive || return 1 fi tls_acme_err "$action: owned resources + systemd readback OK。" ) tls_acme_deploy_internal() ( local mode=$1 root info out tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 root=$(tls_acme_root) || return 1 if [[ $mode == authorized ]]; then tls_acme_fs hook ready || return 1 # Fixed ownedhook, not a Certbot shell hook or external path. Check source # and old manifest again inside the isolated child before any service write. /bin/bash "$root/vmess-hook.sh" || return 1 else declare -F toolbox_vmess >/dev/null && declare -F vmess_fs >/dev/null || { tls_acme_err 'VMess update-cert ownership API 尚未加载；不直接覆盖 cert/key/manifest。'; return 1; } info=$(vmess_fs verify --complete) || { tls_acme_err 'VMess 旧 manifest 验证失败。'; return 1; } local host _port _ws _listen hasunit cert key read -r host _port _ws _listen hasunit <<< "$info" [[ $host == "$tls_acme_host" && $hasunit == 1 ]] || { tls_acme_err 'VMess hostname/完整归属不匹配。'; return 1; } out=$(tls_acme_fs cert) || return 1 mapfile -t tls_acme_cert <<< "$out"; cert=${tls_acme_cert[0]}; key=${tls_acme_cert[1]} toolbox_vmess update-cert "$cert" "$key" || return 1 vmess_fs verify --complete >/dev/null || return 1 fi ) tls_acme_hook_install() { local hook line name value root vmess_source network_source f local -a vmess_functions=() network_functions=() root=$(tls_acme_root) || return 1 # Freeze loaded functions from the actual source package/bundle; source path # does not exist for bash -s and is not needed. Names are allowlisted, values # are quoted shell data. Source SHA-256 is sealed in the owned tree. while IFS= read -r f; do case $f in vmess_*|toolbox_vmess) vmess_functions+=("$f");; net_err|net_die|net_check_cert|net_tls_verify|net_tls_trusted|net_a5_sha256|net_a5_zip_url|net_alpine_xray_sha256|net_xray_asset_arch) network_functions+=("$f");; esac done < <(compgen -A function) [[ ${#vmess_functions[@]} -gt 1 && ${#network_functions[@]} -gt 3 ]] || { tls_acme_err '缺少已加载 VMess/network source functions。'; return 1; } vmess_source=$(declare -f "${vmess_functions[@]}") || return 1 network_source=$(declare -f "${network_functions[@]}") || return 1 while IFS= read -r name; do case $name in NET_*|TOOLBOX_A5_SHA256|TOOLBOX_A5_SHA256_ARM64) # Preserve scalar constants referenced by shared functions; no arrays # and no export/readonly flags which might interfere with re-sourcing. value=${!name}; printf -v line '%s=%q\n' "$name" "$value"; network_source="$line$network_source";; esac done < <(compgen -A variable) hook=$'#!/bin/bash\nset -u\numask 077\n' printf -v line 'source %q\n' "$root/runtime.sh"; hook+="$line" hook+=$'tls_acme_fs hook ready || exit 1\ntls_acme_info || exit 1\n' # Allowlisted configuration is shell-quoted, never a command/hook argument. for name in TOOLBOX_VM_WS_ETC TOOLBOX_VM_WS_BIN_DIR TOOLBOX_VM_WS_UNIT_DIR TOOLBOX_VM_WS_LOCK TOOLBOX_VM_WS_OS_RELEASE TOOLBOX_VM_WS_SYSTEMD_RUN TOOLBOX_VM_WS_LISTEN TOOLBOX_NET_CAFILE TOOLBOX_NET_CAPATH TMPDIR; do if [[ ${!name+x} ]]; then value=${!name}; printf -v line '%s=%q\nexport %s\n' "$name" "$value" "$name"; hook+="$line"; fi done printf -v line 'source %q || exit 1\nsource %q || exit 1\n' "$root/network-source.sh" "$root/vmess-source.sh"; hook+="$line" hook+=$'info=$(vmess_fs verify --complete) || exit 1\nread -r host _port _ws _listen hasunit <<< "$info"\n[[ $host == "$tls_acme_host" && $hasunit == 1 ]] || exit 1\n' hook+=$'out=$(tls_acme_fs cert) || exit 1\nmapfile -t pair <<< "$out"\n' # VMess keeps its transaction and public API, including manifest/service/TLS # checks. Its consent callback is substituted ONLY in this fixed ownedhook # child after the TLS module's dual YES and sealed authorization readback. hook+=$'vmess_confirm() { tls_acme_fs hook ready; }\ntoolbox_vmess update-cert "${pair[0]}" "${pair[1]}" || exit 1\nvmess_fs verify --complete >/dev/null || exit 1\n' tls_acme_fs hook-install "$vmess_source" "$network_source" "$hook" } tls_acme_deploy() ( local host=${1:-} tls_acme_lock_id='' tls_acme_version='' tls_acme_binary='' tls_acme_host='' tls_acme_email='' info vmhost _port _ws _listen hasunit tls_acme_require && tls_acme_info "$host" || return 1 tls_acme_fs cert >/dev/null || return 1 declare -F toolbox_vmess >/dev/null && declare -F vmess_fs >/dev/null || { tls_acme_err '缺少 VMess ownership update-cert API。'; return 1; } info=$(vmess_fs verify --complete) || return 1 read -r vmhost _port _ws _listen hasunit <<< "$info" [[ $vmhost == "$tls_acme_host" && $hasunit == 1 ]] || { tls_acme_err 'VMess hostname/旧完整 manifest 不匹配。'; return 1; } tls_acme_err '预览：预授权现在及后续成功续期调用 VMess update-cert（短暂停服，旧 manifest/hostname/链/time/keypair/TLS+WS readback）；保存 source package snapshot 与固定 ownedhook；不传任意 shell hook，不直接覆盖 cert/key。' tls_acme_err 'A1/A3/A4 没有同等 certpair+sealed manifest+service rollback API，因此明确不自动部署。启用此授权不自动启用 timer；disable-renewal 撤销后台续期但保留此 VMess 同步策略。' tls_acme_confirm || return 1 tls_acme_lock_id=$(tls_acme_fs lock) || return 1 trap tls_acme_cleanup EXIT trap 'exit 130' INT; trap 'exit 143' TERM HUP tls_acme_info "$host" || return 1 tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 if [[ ${tls_acme_meta[4]} != yes ]]; then tls_acme_hook_install && tls_acme_fs vmess || return 1 fi tls_acme_lock_check || return 1 tls_acme_deploy_internal authorized || { tls_acme_err 'VMess deployment 失败；保留授权/source/日志，VMess API 自行保守回滚。'; return 1; } tls_acme_info "$host" && tls_acme_fs hook ready || return 1 tls_acme_err 'VMess 证书 update-cert API 完成；ownedhook/manifest readback OK，后续成功续期已预授权同步。' ) toolbox_tls_acme() { local action=${1:-status}; if (( $# )); then shift; fi case $action in issue) tls_acme_issue "$@";; status) [[ $# -le 1 ]] || return 2; tls_acme_dependencies python3 || return 1; tls_acme_fs status "${1:-}";; renew) [[ $# -le 1 ]] || return 2; tls_acme_renew no "${1:-}";; _renew) [[ $# == 1 ]] || return 2; tls_acme_renew yes "$1";; enable-renewal|disable-renewal) [[ $# -le 1 ]] || return 2; tls_acme_timer "$action" "${1:-}";; deploy-vmess) [[ $# -le 1 ]] || return 2; tls_acme_deploy "${1:-}";; *) tls_acme_err '用法: toolbox_tls_acme issue [hostname] [email] | status [hostname] | renew [hostname] | enable-renewal [hostname] | disable-renewal [hostname] | deploy-vmess [hostname]'; return 2;; esac } # END bundled module: tls-acme # BEGIN bundled module: reinstall-provenance #!/usr/bin/env bash # Source-only provenance. Pin updates require human review of upstream and this diff. _TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_COMMIT=54fae2067557b3c0ca49e7c9ce03bca63c793f8c _TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_RAW_SHA=3ca358bf065ebd3ecb2f727e152a4b4fb9b154398abc1b9a5fe0b599f1027d7a _TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_DERIVED_SHA=f51939ced0634c1a12f429e94a58e10c7b939b88ff4ea3ca9240f748b90be663 # README at this commit, lines 705..719, documents pinning confhome* to commit_id. # Reviewed derivation: both active origins use SAME immutable GitHub commit; # remove upstream curl --insecure from download and URL probe (two exact # occurrences), use HTTPS for Alpine boot images and Debian driver packages # injected into the initrd. Add the adapter-only fixed Linux/root SHA512-file # path, skip upstream plaintext prompts/fallback hashing/credential echo there. # Installation, bootloader and runtime auth application remain upstream-owned. # trans.sh + initrd-network.sh are downloaded through that origin; extra_confhome # propagates it into initrd/runtime. Distribution images/packages remain mutable. _toolbox_reinstall_derive() { python3 - "$1" "$2" "$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_COMMIT" "$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_RAW_SHA" "$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_DERIVED_SHA" <<'PY' import hashlib, os, sys src, dst, commit, raw_sha, derived_sha = sys.argv[1:] b = open(src, 'rb').read() if hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest() != raw_sha: sys.exit('上游原始 SHA256 不匹配；未执行。') edits = [ (b'confhome=https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bin456789/reinstall/main\n', ('confhome=https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bin456789/reinstall/'+commit+'\n').encode()), (b'confhome_cn=https://cnb.cool/bin456789/reinstall/-/git/raw/main\n', ('confhome_cn=https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bin456789/reinstall/'+commit+'\n').encode()), (b'command curl --insecure --connect-timeout 10 -f "$@"', b'command curl --connect-timeout 10 -f "$@"'), (b'command curl --insecure --connect-timeout 10 -Lfr 0-1048575 "$url"', b'command curl --connect-timeout 10 -Lfr 0-1048575 "$url"'), (b'mirror=http://mirror.nju.edu.cn/alpine/v$releasever', b'mirror=https://mirror.nju.edu.cn/alpine/v$releasever'), (b'mirror=http://dl-cdn.alpinelinux.org/alpine/v$releasever', b'mirror=https://dl-cdn.alpinelinux.org/alpine/v$releasever'), (b'local url=http://$mirror/dists/$nextos_codename/main/binary-$basearch_alt/Packages.gz', b'local url=https://$mirror/dists/$nextos_codename/main/binary-$basearch_alt/Packages.gz'), (b'local url=http://$mirror/dists/$nextos_codename/main/debian-installer/binary-$basearch_alt/Packages.gz', b'local url=https://$mirror/dists/$nextos_codename/main/debian-installer/binary-$basearch_alt/Packages.gz'), (b'curl -Lo $tmp/tmp.deb http://$mirror/"$deb_path"', b'curl -Lo $tmp/tmp.deb https://$mirror/"$deb_path"'), (b'is_in_china && mirror=http://mirror.nju.edu.cn/alpine || mirror=https://dl-cdn.alpinelinux.org/alpine', b'is_in_china && mirror=https://mirror.nju.edu.cn/alpine || mirror=https://dl-cdn.alpinelinux.org/alpine'), # Adapter-only hashed-password interface: no plaintext argv/env, no upstream # prompt/random password/hash fallback or credential echo on this path. (b'set -eE\n', b'set -eE\n# Adapter secrets must never inherit tracing/debug hooks.\nset +x\nset +T\ntrap - DEBUG RETURN ERR\ntoolbox_password_hash_file=\ntoolbox_password_hash=\n'), (b' passwd: password: \\\n', b' passwd: password: toolbox-password-hash-file: \\\n'), (b' --passwd | --password)\n', b' --toolbox-password-hash-file)\n toolbox_password_hash_file=$2\n shift 2\n ;;\n --passwd | --password)\n'), (b'prompt_password() {\n', b'''# Read only the adapter's exclusive owned0600 sibling, never arbitrary input. toolbox_load_password_hash() { toolbox_password_hash=$(python3 - "$toolbox_password_hash_file" "$THIS_SCRIPT" <<'TOOLBOX_HASH_PY' import os,re,stat,sys p,script=sys.argv[1:] def fail():sys.exit('Adapter password hash file refused') if p!=os.path.dirname(script)+'/password.sha512':fail() for i in range(1,len(p.split('/'))): q='/'.join(p.split('/')[:i]) or '/';s=os.lstat(q) if not stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode) or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or s.st_mode&0o022:fail() s=os.lstat(p) if not stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) or s.st_nlink!=1 or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)!=0o600:fail() f=os.open(p,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_NONBLOCK) try: h=os.fstat(f) if (h.st_dev,h.st_ino,h.st_mode,h.st_uid,h.st_nlink)!=(s.st_dev,s.st_ino,s.st_mode,s.st_uid,s.st_nlink):fail() b=os.read(f,257) finally:os.close(f) if not re.fullmatch(rb'\\$6\\$[./A-Za-z0-9]{1,16}\\$[./A-Za-z0-9]{86}\\n',b):fail() sys.stdout.buffer.write(b) TOOLBOX_HASH_PY ) || error_and_exit "Adapter password hash file refused" } prompt_password() { '''), ('# 检查必须的参数\nverify_os_args\n'.encode(), '''# Adapter mode is only reviewed for these fixed Linux targets/root user. if [ -n "$toolbox_password_hash_file" ]; then case "$distro $releasever" in 'debian 13'|'ubuntu 24.04'|'alpine 3.24') ;; *) error_and_exit "Adapter password target refused";; esac [ "$username" = root ] && [ -z "$password" ] && [ -z "$ssh_keys" ] || error_and_exit "Adapter authentication conflict" toolbox_load_password_hash fi # 检查必须的参数 verify_os_args '''.encode()), (b'if [ -z "$password" ] && [ -z "$ssh_keys" ]; then\n', b'if [ -z "$password" ] && [ -z "$ssh_keys" ] && [ -z "$toolbox_password_hash" ]; then\n'), (b' cat <<<"$ssh_keys" >$initrd_dir/configs/ssh_keys\n else\n', b''' cat <<<"$ssh_keys" >$initrd_dir/configs/ssh_keys elif [ -n "$toolbox_password_hash_file" ]; then toolbox_load_password_hash (umask 077; builtin printf '%s\\n' "$toolbox_password_hash" >"$initrd_dir/configs/password-linux-sha512") unset toolbox_password_hash else '''), (''' # 普通 linux echo "Username: $username" if [ -n "$ssh_keys" ]; then echo "Public Key: $ssh_keys" else echo "Password: $password" fi '''.encode(), ''' # 普通 linux echo "Username: $username" if [ -n "$ssh_keys" ]; then echo "Public Key: $ssh_keys" elif [ -n "$toolbox_password_hash_file" ]; then echo "Authentication: password (credential hidden)" else echo "Password: $password" fi '''.encode())] for old, new in edits: if b.count(old) != 1: sys.exit('pin patch 前置条件不匹配；需要显式 review。') b = b.replace(old, new) if hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest() != derived_sha: sys.exit('派生 SHA256 不匹配；未执行。') fd = os.open(dst, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600) with os.fdopen(fd, 'wb') as f: f.write(b) PY } _toolbox_reinstall_download() { curl --proto '=https' --proto-redir '=https' --tlsv1.2 --fail --location \ --connect-timeout 15 --max-time 120 --output "$2" \ "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bin456789/reinstall/$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_COMMIT/$1" } _toolbox_reinstall_fetch() { local dir=$1 name digest actual _toolbox_reinstall_download reinstall.sh "$dir/upstream.raw" || return _toolbox_reinstall_derive "$dir/upstream.raw" "$dir/reinstall.sh" || return # These executable helpers are checked AGAIN from unpacked initrd before a # plan is declared prepared. Other repo resources inherit the same commit. for name in trans.sh initrd-network.sh; do case $name in trans.sh) digest=d8f34529c0a706b17d67965b99a107f8299a30d5303744a20f0fb96556365bf7;; initrd-network.sh) digest=78fd74716b236d0e41e4f5c4034d45db8bcc47fba2d53c4a19a0d21ef60510bc;; esac _toolbox_reinstall_download "$name" "$dir/$name" || return actual=$(sha256sum "$dir/$name") || return [[ ${actual%% *} == "$digest" ]] || { printf 'runtime helper SHA256 不匹配：%s\n' "$name" >&2; return 1; } done bash -n "$dir/reinstall.sh" || return _toolbox_reinstall_expected_trans "$dir" || return } _toolbox_reinstall_expected_trans() { local dir=$1 # Reuse the exact upstream Debian initrd transformation, statically extracted # only after raw SHA validation. Execute its sed/insert functions on PRIVATE # fixture files; never source or enter the full installer to discover them. python3 - "$dir" "$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_RAW_SHA" <<'PY' || return import hashlib,os,sys p,sha=sys.argv[1:];b=open(p+'/upstream.raw','rb').read() if hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()!=sha:sys.exit('raw hash changed before trans derivation') s=b.decode().splitlines(True) fragment=''.join(s[358:392])+''.join(s[4182:4209]) # Reviewed source boundaries: insert_into_file + only Debian trans modifications. if not fragment.startswith('insert_into_file() {') or not fragment.endswith(' $initrd_dir/trans.sh\n'):sys.exit('upstream fragment boundaries changed') os.mkdir(p+'/trans-derive',0o700) with open(p+'/trans-derive/trans.sh','xb') as f:f.write(open(p+'/trans.sh','rb').read()) with open(p+'/trans-transform.sh','x') as f: f.write('initrd_dir="$1"\nnextos_distro=debian\nnextos_releasever=13\nreplace=\'\\\\n: #\'\n'+fragment) PY bash "$dir/trans-transform.sh" "$dir/trans-derive" || return mv "$dir/trans-derive/trans.sh" "$dir/expected-debian-trans.sh" || return rmdir "$dir/trans-derive" || return rm "$dir/trans-transform.sh" || return } # END bundled module: reinstall-provenance # BEGIN bundled module: reinstall #!/usr/bin/env bash # Source-only M3 adapter; REAL bin456789/reinstall owns installation. # This adapter never calls reboot. Upstream's initrd DOES reboot itself once the # install finishes (default hold semantics); no --hold is passed (no re-review). # Parent build must load reinstall-provenance.sh before this module. # Remember the source directory before the caller changes cwd. Bash reports only # a basename for `source reinstall.sh` from the module's own directory. if [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} != 1 ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_module_dir=$( _toolbox_reinstall_source=${BASH_SOURCE[0]:-} if [[ $_toolbox_reinstall_source == */* ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_source=${_toolbox_reinstall_source%/*}; else _toolbox_reinstall_source=.; fi CDPATH= builtin cd -- "$_toolbox_reinstall_source" && builtin pwd -P ) fi _toolbox_reinstall_error() { printf '%s\n' "$*" >&2; } _toolbox_reinstall_has() { command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; } _toolbox_reinstall_root() { [[ ${EUID:-1} == 0 ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '需要 root；未执行。'; return 1; }; } _toolbox_reinstall_state_dir() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_STATE:-/var/lib/terry-toolbox/reinstall}"; } _toolbox_reinstall_host_root() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_ROOT:-/}"; } _toolbox_reinstall_need() { local c missing=() for c in "$@"; do _toolbox_reinstall_has "$c" || missing+=("$c"); done (( ${#missing[@]} != 0 )) || return 0 # Normal menu/bundle entry loads the shared core. Standalone source remains # side-effect free; load its sibling only when commands really are missing. if ! declare -F toolbox_require_commands >/dev/null; then local core="${_toolbox_reinstall_module_dir:-}/dependencies.sh" [[ ${TOOLBOX_BUNDLED:-0} != 1 && -n ${_toolbox_reinstall_module_dir:-} ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '打包入口未加载共享依赖模块，未执行。'; return 1; } [[ -r $core ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error "缺少依赖：${missing[*]}；共享依赖模块不可用，未执行。"; return 1; } source "$core" || return fi toolbox_require_commands 'M3 重装普通工具' "${missing[@]}" || return # Keep the caller's own probe authoritative (also used by offline fixtures). for c in "${missing[@]}"; do _toolbox_reinstall_has "$c" || { _toolbox_reinstall_error "依赖安装后仍缺少命令：$c；未执行。"; return 1; } done _toolbox_reinstall_error 'M3 普通工具依赖已按确认安装；此步骤修改了系统软件包，cancel 不会卸载这些包。磁盘/引导预检仍只读，不安装或转换 GRUB/init。' } _toolbox_reinstall_need_bootloader() { local c missing=() for c in "$@"; do _toolbox_reinstall_has "$c" || missing+=("$c"); done (( ${#missing[@]} == 0 )) || { _toolbox_reinstall_error "缺少现有 BIOS GRUB 命令：${missing[*]}；不会自动安装或转换引导器/init，不接管当前引导，未执行重装 prepare。" return 1 } } _toolbox_reinstall_container() { local r rc=0 out line vm=0 r=$(_toolbox_reinstall_host_root) [[ ! -e $r/.dockerenv && ! -e $r/run/.containerenv && ! -d $r/proc/vz ]] || return 0 # systemd-detect-virt is NOT required: Alpine/musl hosts normally lack it. # Fall back to virt-what (already a prepare dependency) and DMI evidence, so a # plain OpenRC VM is not misread as "container state unknown". if _toolbox_reinstall_has systemd-detect-virt; then systemd-detect-virt --container --quiet || rc=$? case $rc in 0) return 0;; 1) return 1;; esac elif _toolbox_reinstall_has virt-what; then out=$(virt-what 2>/dev/null) || return 2 while IFS= read -r line; do case $line in docker|podman|lxc|openvz|systemd-nspawn*|container*) return 0;; kvm|vmware|virtualbox|xen|hyperv|qemu|microsoft|amazon|google|bhyve|parallels|oracle) vm=1;; esac done <<<"$out" # virt-what may report a nested container and the underlying hypervisor. # Container evidence always wins, independent of line order. [[ $vm != 1 ]] || return 1 fi python3 - "$r" <<'PY' import os, sys r=sys.argv[1] try: c=open(r+'/proc/1/cgroup').read() if any(x in c.lower() for x in ('docker','kubepods','containerd','lxc','libpod')): sys.exit(0) except OSError: pass # Firmware/DMI presence is real bare-metal/VM evidence; absence stays unknown. try: if open(r+'/sys/class/dmi/id/product_name').read().strip(): sys.exit(1) except OSError: pass sys.exit(2) PY } _toolbox_reinstall_init() { # PID1 identity decides the supported init, never detector presence. # systemd => Debian/Ubuntu only; OpenRC => Alpine only; anything else unknown. local r comm='' r=$(_toolbox_reinstall_host_root) comm=$(command cat "$r/proc/1/comm" 2>/dev/null) || comm='' comm=${comm%%$'\n'*} case $comm in systemd) printf 'systemd\n';; init|openrc-init|busybox) # OpenRC needs positive state, not just a generic "init" comm # (sysvinit Debian is also "init" and stays unsupported). Upstream # prepares the bootloader on this host and installs a different OS after boot. local rc=0 arch inventory mounts efi='' r disk secure init want=${1:-} commands=() case $want in ''|debian|ubuntu|alpine) ;; *) _toolbox_reinstall_error "preflight 目标未审定: $want"; return 1;; esac _toolbox_reinstall_root || return r=$(_toolbox_reinstall_host_root) commands=(bash python3 curl sha256sum ssh-keygen lsblk findmnt flock uname stat cp mv rm mkdir rmdir env cat id grep sed awk sort head tail wc cut tr xargs find readlink fdisk gzip cpio lsmem dmidecode virt-what) # Gather mode-specific ordinary tools before asking once. Do not log password argv. env -i HOME=/root PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin \ LC_ALL=C TERM="${TERM:-dumb}" bash "$1" "${@:2}" &2 if ! IFS= read -r answer || [[ ${answer^^} != YES ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_error '已取消/EOF（必须完整 newline）'; return 1; fi printf '确认下一次人工重启会清除上述整个磁盘；再次输入 YES: ' >&2 if ! IFS= read -r answer || [[ ${answer^^} != YES ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_error '已取消/EOF（必须完整 newline）'; return 1; fi printf '逐字输入 whole physical disk 路径 %s: ' "$1" >&2 if ! Collect only on the # caller's TTY; read -s and signal traps restore its exact terminal state. _toolbox_reinstall_password() { [[ -t 0 ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '密码必须从交互 TTY 隐藏输入；拒绝管道/EOF'; return 1; } _toolbox_reinstall_need openssl stty || return local LC_ALL=C auth_first='' auth_second='' tty_state builtin export -n auth_first auth_second _toolbox_reinstall_password_hash tty_state=$(stty -g) || return trap 'stty "$tty_state"; unset auth_first auth_second _toolbox_reinstall_password_hash; exit 130' INT TERM HUP # Disable echo before publishing the prompt: otherwise a fast PTY/client can # send credentials between printf and read -s, leaking them through echo. stty -echo || return printf '密码（隐藏，12–256 字节，保留空格/特殊字符）: ' >&2 if !
