IFS= read -rs auth_second; then stty "$tty_state"; trap - INT TERM HUP _toolbox_reinstall_error '密码确认 EOF；取消'; return 1 fi stty "$tty_state" || return trap - INT TERM HUP printf '\n' >&2 # OpenSSL passwd -stdin truncates beyond 256 bytes on reviewed hosts. Count # bytes (C locale), reject rather than silently changing the entered secret. if [[ -z $auth_first || ${#auth_first} -lt 12 || ${#auth_first} -gt 256 ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_error '密码必须非空且为 12–256 字节；取消'; return 1 fi [[ $auth_first == "$auth_second" ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '两次密码不一致；取消'; return 1; } _toolbox_reinstall_password_hash=$(builtin printf '%s\n' "$auth_first" | command env -i PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin openssl passwd -6 -stdin) || return unset auth_first auth_second [[ $_toolbox_reinstall_password_hash =~ ^\$6\$[./A-Za-z0-9]{1,16}\$[./A-Za-z0-9]{86}$ ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error 'SHA512 密码生成失败；取消'; return 1; } } _toolbox_reinstall_prepare() ( # Disable inherited tracing/hooks BEFORE collecting any secret. This is an # isolated subshell: the caller's tracing options/traps are not changed. set +x set +T trap - DEBUG RETURN ERR umask 077 local distro=${1:-debian} version=${2:-} key state r name target first efi boot rc=0 lockfd lock_identity key_digest copied_digest auth_mode choice _toolbox_reinstall_password_hash='' local -a auth_args=() export -n _toolbox_reinstall_password_hash case $distro in debian) version=${version:-13}; [[ $version == 13 ]] || return 1;; ubuntu) version=${version:-24.04}; [[ $version == 24.04 ]] || return 1;; alpine) version=${version:-3.24}; [[ $version == 3.24 ]] || return 1;; *) _toolbox_reinstall_error '仅固定 debian 13 / ubuntu 24.04 LTS / alpine 3.24'; return 1;; esac [[ $# -le 2 ]] || return 1 _toolbox_reinstall_preflight "$distro" || return state=$(_toolbox_reinstall_state_dir);r=$(_toolbox_reinstall_host_root) if [[ -e $state/active || -L $state/active ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_data archive-check "$r" "$state" || return printf '上次取消已验证；确认后保留其 sealed report 为 cancelled 历史，再创建新计划。\n' fi key=${TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_SSH_KEY_FILE:-} auth_mode=${TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_AUTH_MODE:-} # Preserve existing noninteractive key-file callers: never consume their YES # as a new menu choice. then printf '本地 ssh-ed25519 公钥绝对路径 (.pub): ' >&2 IFS= read -r key || { _toolbox_reinstall_error 'SSH 公钥未输入/EOF'; return 1; } fi _toolbox_reinstall_pubkey "$key" || return key_digest=$(sha256sum "$key") || return key_digest=${key_digest%% *};; *) _toolbox_reinstall_error '认证模式仅允许 password/key；取消'; return 1;; esac name=$(python3 -c 'import json,sys;print(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["name"])' "$_toolbox_reinstall_disk") || return target="$distro $version";first=$_toolbox_reinstall_disk printf 'Authentication: %s（root 登录；只启用所选模式）\n' "$auth_mode" printf 'Target: %s；上游 commit: %s\n' "$target" "$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_COMMIT" printf '精确 boot 范围: /reinstall-{vmlinuz,initrd,firmware}, /reinstall-tmp, BIOS /boot/grub{,2}/{custom.cfg,grub.cfg,grubenv} 或唯一 ESP/EFI/reinstall；/etc/default/kexec。\n' printf '上游 runtime 可能仅补缺 curl/grep/fdisk/lsblk/findmnt/gzip/cpio/dmidecode/virt-what 等（UEFI efibootmgr、Debian ar/dpkg/xz 等）；包仓库/镜像不是内容 pin，cancel 不卸载包。\n' _toolbox_reinstall_confirm "$name" || return # Recheck topology AND boot conflicts after consent, before writes/fetch. _toolbox_reinstall_preflight "$distro" || return [[ $first == "$_toolbox_reinstall_disk" ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '确认后 disk 身份变化；未执行'; return 1; } python3 - "$state" <<'PY' || return import os,stat,sys p=sys.argv[1] for i in range(1,len(p.split('/'))+1): q='/'.join(p.split('/')[:i]) or '/' if os.path.lexists(q) and stat.S_ISLNK(os.lstat(q).st_mode):sys.exit('state 路径 symlink；拒绝') os.makedirs(p,mode=0o700,exist_ok=True) s=os.lstat(p) if s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)!=0o700:sys.exit('state 权限必须 owned 0700') PY python3 - "$state/lock" <<'PY' || return import os,stat,sys p=sys.argv[1] try: s=os.lstat(p) if not stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) or s.st_nlink!=1 or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)!=0o600:sys.exit('lock 身份不可信') except FileNotFoundError: fd=os.open(p,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);os.close(fd) PY exec {lockfd}<"$state/lock" || return flock -n "$lockfd" || { _toolbox_reinstall_error 'concurrent reinstall 操作；拒绝'; return 1; } lock_identity=$(_toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd") || return if [[ -e $state/active || -L $state/active ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_data archive "$r" "$state" || return fi mkdir "$state/active" || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '已有 plan（竞争）；拒绝'; return 1; } # Keep failures/signals with reports. NEVER auto-reset an uncertain plan. trap '_toolbox_reinstall_error "收到信号；保留 report，本工具不自动 reset/reboot"; if _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null && [[ -f $state/active/seal ]]; then _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" interrupted "信号中断；人工检查 boot 与日志"; fi; exit 130' INT TERM HUP if [[ $auth_mode == key ]]; then cp -- "$key" "$state/active/key.pub" || return _toolbox_reinstall_pubkey "$state/active/key.pub" || return copied_digest=$(sha256sum "$state/active/key.pub") || return [[ ${copied_digest%% *} == "$key_digest" ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '确认的 SSH 公钥在复制前发生变化；未执行上游，保留 report'; return 1; } auth_args=(--ssh-key "$state/active/key.pub") else # Hash travels only over this dedicated descriptor; exclusive owned0600 # creation is validated before the pinned installer receives its pathname. _toolbox_reinstall_data auth-write "$r" "$state" 3< <(builtin printf '%s\n' "$_toolbox_reinstall_password_hash") || return unset _toolbox_reinstall_password_hash auth_args=(--toolbox-password-hash-file "$state/active/password.sha512") fi _toolbox_reinstall_fetch "$state/active" || { _toolbox_reinstall_error "provenance 失败；保留 $state/active；未执行上游"; return 1; } # Downloads can take time. Do not adopt boot files or topology that raced in. _toolbox_reinstall_preflight "$distro" || return [[ $first == "$_toolbox_reinstall_disk" ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '下载后 disk 身份变化；拒绝上游'; return 1; } _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null || return boot=$(_toolbox_reinstall_boot_id) || return _toolbox_reinstall_data init "$r" "$state" "$target" "$_toolbox_reinstall_disk" "$boot" "$_TOOLBOX_REINSTALL_COMMIT" "$_toolbox_reinstall_efi_before" "$_toolbox_reinstall_host_init" "$auth_mode" || return _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null || return # Print real errors to stderr as well as retain the private log, preserving rc. _toolbox_reinstall_run_upstream "$state/active/reinstall.sh" "$distro" "$version" \ --username root "${auth_args[@]}" --target-disk "$name" \ >"$state/active/prepare.log" 2>&1 || rc=$? command cat "$state/active/prepare.log" >&2 _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null || return if (( rc != 0 )); then _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" failed "上游 prepare exit=$rc；不宣称 done；保留日志与现场" || return return "$rc" fi efi='';[[ ! -d $r/sys/firmware/efi ]] || efi=$(_toolbox_reinstall_efi) || return _toolbox_reinstall_data prepared "$r" "$state" "$efi" || { _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" failed '上游返回 0 但 prepared/readback 验证失败；不可自动 reset' || return; return 1; } printf '仅 prepare 完成并回读；尚未安装，本阶段不会重启。重启交接：先 toolbox_reinstall status，核实备份/rescue 后由操作者重启；进入 initrd 后上游安装完成会自行 reboot（未用 --hold），需自行观察第二次重启结果——本工具从不执行 reboot。\n' ) _toolbox_reinstall_status() { local r state efi='' boot commands=(python3 cat) r=$(_toolbox_reinstall_host_root);state=$(_toolbox_reinstall_state_dir) [[ -e $state/active || -L $state/active ]] || { printf '没有本工具 owned plan；只读，未调用上游。\n'; return 0; } [[ ! -d $r/sys/firmware/efi ]] || commands+=(efibootmgr) _toolbox_reinstall_need "${commands[@]}" || return [[ -f $state/active/seal && ! -L $state/active ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error "blocking: unsealed/unknown plan $state/active；下载/准备中断；保留 report，禁止 reset"; return 1; } boot=$(_toolbox_reinstall_boot_id) || return [[ ! -d $r/sys/firmware/efi ]] || efi=$(_toolbox_reinstall_efi) || return _toolbox_reinstall_data status "$r" "$state" "$boot" "$efi" } _toolbox_reinstall_cancel() ( umask 077 local r state efi='' boot rc=0 lockfd init lock_identity commands=(python3 flock uname lsblk findmnt bash sha256sum cat) _toolbox_reinstall_root || return r=$(_toolbox_reinstall_host_root);state=$(_toolbox_reinstall_state_dir) [[ ! -d $r/sys/firmware/efi ]] || commands+=(efibootmgr) _toolbox_reinstall_need "${commands[@]}" || return init=$(_toolbox_reinstall_init) || return 1 local container_rc=0 _toolbox_reinstall_container || container_rc=$? [[ $container_rc == 1 ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error 'cancel container/unknown state；拒绝 reset'; return 1; } [[ -e $state/active/seal && -f $state/lock && ! -L $state/lock ]] || { _toolbox_reinstall_error '没有可验证 owned plan；拒绝调用上游 reset'; return 1; } python3 - "$state/lock" <<'PY' || return import os,stat,sys s=os.lstat(sys.argv[1]) if not stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) or s.st_nlink!=1 or s.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)!=0o600:sys.exit('cancel lock 身份不可信') PY exec {lockfd}<"$state/lock" || return flock -n "$lockfd" || { _toolbox_reinstall_error 'concurrent 操作；拒绝 reset'; return 1; } lock_identity=$(_toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd") || return boot=$(_toolbox_reinstall_boot_id) || return [[ ! -d $r/sys/firmware/efi ]] || efi=$(_toolbox_reinstall_efi) || return local current_disk inventory mounts arch inventory=$(_toolbox_reinstall_lsblk) || return mounts=$(_toolbox_reinstall_mounts) || return arch=$(uname -m) || return current_disk=$(_toolbox_reinstall_data inventory "$r" "$state" "$inventory" "$mounts" "$arch") || return _toolbox_reinstall_data verify "$r" "$state" "$boot" "$efi" "$current_disk" "$init" || return # Refuse reset if a mount appeared inside its rm -rf workspace. _toolbox_reinstall_mounts | python3 -c 'import json,sys;d=json.load(sys.stdin);q=list(d["filesystems"]) while q: n=q.pop();q+=n.get("children",[]) if n["target"].startswith("/reinstall-tmp"):sys.exit("workspace 新增 mount；拒绝 reset")' || return trap 'if _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null; then _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" cancel-interrupted "reset 信号中断；保留现场"; fi; exit 130' INT TERM HUP _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null || return _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" cancelling '即将仅 reset 已验证 owned plan' || return _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null || return _toolbox_reinstall_run_upstream "$state/active/reinstall.sh" reset >"$state/active/cancel.log" 2>&1 || rc=$? command cat "$state/active/cancel.log" >&2 _toolbox_reinstall_lock_guard "$state" "$lockfd" "$lock_identity" >/dev/null || return if (( rc != 0 )); then _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" reset-failed "上游 reset exit=$rc；不恢复/删除；保留 report" || return return "$rc" fi efi='';[[ ! -d $r/sys/firmware/efi ]] || efi=$(_toolbox_reinstall_efi) || return _toolbox_reinstall_data restore "$r" "$state" "$efi" || { _toolbox_reinstall_data phase "$r" "$state" restore-failed 'reset 后 bootstate/精确恢复回读失败；保留 report' || return; return 1; } _toolbox_reinstall_status ) toolbox_reinstall() { case ${1:-preflight} in preflight) [[ $# -le 2 ]] || return 1; _toolbox_reinstall_preflight "${2:-}";; prepare) shift; _toolbox_reinstall_prepare "$@";; status) [[ $# == 1 ]] || return 1; _toolbox_reinstall_status;; cancel) [[ $# == 1 ]] || return 1; _toolbox_reinstall_cancel;; *) _toolbox_reinstall_error '用法: toolbox_reinstall preflight [debian|ubuntu|alpine] | prepare [debian|ubuntu|alpine] [version] | status | cancel'; return 1;; esac } # END bundled module: reinstall run_action() { local key=$1 case "$key" in DIAG) toolbox_diagnostics ;; A1|A2|A3|A4|A5|B12|B23|B31) toolbox_network "$key" ;; B3|B4|B5|B9|B13|B17|B20|B21|B22|B24|B25|B26|B27|B28|B29|B30) toolbox_app "${key#B}" ;; *) printf '无效选项：%s\n' "$key" ; return 1 ;; esac } _tb_managed_key() { case "$1" in A[1-5]|B1|B10|B12|B16|B19|B23|B31|MAIN_REINSTALL|TLS) return 0;; *) return 1;; esac } _toolbox_webssh_container() { declare -F toolbox_webssh_container >/dev/null || { printf 'WebSSH 容器模块未加载。\n' >&2; return 1; } toolbox_webssh_container "$@" } _toolbox_tls_acme() { declare -F toolbox_tls_acme >/dev/null || { printf 'TLS ACME 模块未加载。\n' >&2; return 1; } toolbox_tls_acme "$@" } _tb_reinstall_target() { local choice printf '重装目标：1 Debian 13；2 Ubuntu 24.04；3 Alpine 3.24；0 取消\n选择：' >&2 IFS= read -r choice || { printf '输入结束，已取消。\n' >&2; return 1; } case "$choice" in 1) printf 'debian 13\n';; 2) printf 'ubuntu 24.04\n';; 3) printf 'alpine 3.24\n';; *) printf '已取消。\n' >&2; return 1;; esac } _tb_service_screen() { local title case "$1" in A1) title='A1 Alpine Hysteria 2';; _tb_row '↩️ 0. 返回上一层'; _tb_bot printf '\n' } _tb_service_choice_valid() { case "$1" in A5) [[ $2 =~ ^([1-9]|10)$ ]];; B1|MAIN_REINSTALL) [[ $2 =~ ^[1-4]$ ]];; B10) [[ $2 =~ ^[1-9]$ ]];; TLS) [[ $2 =~ ^[1-6]$ ]];; else toolbox_network "$key" "$action"; fi ;; esac } menu() { local choice screen=main key booted=0 service='' parent=main notice='' _tb_ui_init while :; do _tb_redraw case "$screen" in main) _tb_main_screen;;
